-- ======================= CLAUDE CLIENT v1.0 (на базе Hexagram v8, стиль Claude Code) =======================
-- Устройства: ПК, планшет, телефон — интерфейс сам подбирает размер и показывает экранные кнопки на сенсоре.
-- ПК:  RightShift — скрыть/показать окно · F — действие режима · G — ехать к прицелу · X — отмена Бобика
-- Сенсор: круглая кнопка-логотип слева — открыть окно · кнопка действия справа · стрелки/прицел/стоп слева при катании
-- Что нового в v7:
--  • Весь интерфейс без эмодзи: белые линейные иконки рисуются прямо в скрипте (не нужны картинки и asset id)
--  • Окно как у Oblivion: иконки слева, поиск сверху, две колонки, сворачиваемые секции, тонкие переключатели
--  • «Моя ракета»: +15 новых режимов (Атом, Смерч, Корона, Уроборос, Павлин, Галактика, Фонтан, Сердце,
--    Звезда, Планеты, Жонглёр, Куб, ДНК, Пчёлы, Маятник)
local __ok, __err = xpcall(function() -- защита: если что-то сломается, ошибка будет на экране
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local GuiService = game:GetService("GuiService")
local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
local CoreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")

local SHOW_INTRO = true -- поставь false, если интро не нужно

local player = Players.LocalPlayer
local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
local TAU = math.pi * 2

-- убираем старую копию: полностью выгружаем прошлый запуск (иначе его подключения клавиш и событий
-- оставались жить и каждое нажатие срабатывало дважды)
pcall(function() if _G.ClaudeClientStop then _G.ClaudeClientStop() end end)
pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("FembiixMain") end)
pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("FembiixRocket") end)
for _, parent in ipairs({CoreGui, player:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")}) do
	pcall(function()
		for _, n in ipairs({"FembiixGui", "FembiixBox", "Hexagram", "ClaudeClient", "ClaudeLoader"}) do
			local o = parent:FindFirstChild(n)
			if o then o:Destroy() end
		end
	end)
end
pcall(function()
	for _, o in ipairs(camera:GetChildren()) do
		if o.Name:sub(1, 7) == "Fembiix" then o:Destroy() end
	end
	local o = workspace:FindFirstChild("FembiixBoxPart")
	if o then o:Destroy() end
end)

-- ============================ ХЕЛПЕРЫ ============================
local C = { -- светлая тема Claude: кремово-белый фон, тёмный текст, терракотовый акцент
	bg = Color3.fromRGB(250, 249, 245), panel = Color3.fromRGB(240, 238, 232),
	el = Color3.fromRGB(232, 229, 221), el2 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 216, 207),
	accent = Color3.fromRGB(201, 100, 66), accentDark = Color3.fromRGB(242, 205, 189),
	green = Color3.fromRGB(226, 223, 214), danger = Color3.fromRGB(243, 207, 201),
	text = Color3.fromRGB(41, 38, 34), dim = Color3.fromRGB(112, 107, 99),
	off = Color3.fromRGB(214, 210, 201), gold = Color3.fromRGB(201, 100, 66), white = Color3.fromRGB(28, 26, 23),
	icon = Color3.fromRGB(64, 60, 54), iconDim = Color3.fromRGB(150, 145, 137), line = Color3.fromRGB(40, 32, 26),
}
local SPARK_C1, SPARK_C2 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 205, 80), Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 0)

local function make(class, props, parent)
	local o = Instance.new(class)
	for k, v in pairs(props or {}) do o[k] = v end
	if parent then o.Parent = parent end
	return o
end
local function round(o, r) return make("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, r)}, o) end
local function stroke(o, color, th, tr)
	return make("UIStroke", {Color = color, Thickness = th or 1, Transparency = tr or 0, ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border}, o)
end
local function tween(o, props, t, style, dir, rep, rev)
	local tw = TweenService:Create(o, TweenInfo.new(t or 0.15, style or Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, dir or Enum.EasingDirection.Out, rep or 0, rev or false), props)
	tw:Play()
	return tw
end
local UIK = {W = 900, H = 560, thv = 0, SW = 150}
function UIK.lum(c) return 0.299 * c.R + 0.587 * c.G + 0.114 * c.B end
function UIK.txt(bg) return (UIK.lum(bg) > 0.62) and Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 24) or C.text end

-- ============================ ЗАГРУЗЧИК v1.0 ============================
-- Показывается сразу при запуске, поверх всего: логотип Claude, название и версия, прогресс-бар с процентами
-- и текущим этапом. UIK.load(доля, "этап…") вызывается по ходу запуска, UIK.loadDone(дальше) плавно убирает экран.
UIK.VERSION = "v1.0"
do
	local L = {p = 0, shown = 0}
	UIK.loader = L
	local g = make("ScreenGui", {Name = "ClaudeLoader", IgnoreGuiInset = true, DisplayOrder = 200, ResetOnSpawn = false, ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling})
	pcall(function() g.Parent = CoreGui end)
	if not g.Parent then g.Parent = player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui") end
	L.gui = g
	local vp = camera.ViewportSize
	local back = make("CanvasGroup", {Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, GroupTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0}, g) -- без фона на весь экран
	L.back = back
	local holder = make("Frame", {AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.55), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(440, 196), BackgroundTransparency = 1}, back)
	L.scale = make("UIScale", {Scale = math.clamp(math.min(vp.X / 720, vp.Y / 520), 0.6, 1.25)}, holder)
	L.holder = holder
	for i = 1, 3 do -- мягкая тень под карточкой
		local sh = make("Frame", {AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 6 + i * 2), Size = UDim2.new(1, i * 12, 1, i * 12),
			BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 0.94, BorderSizePixel = 0}, holder)
		round(sh, 16 + i * 6)
	end
	local card = make("Frame", {Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(24, 22, 20), BackgroundTransparency = 0.04, BorderSizePixel = 0}, holder)
	round(card, 16)
	stroke(card, C.accent, 1.5, 0.55)
	make("UIGradient", {Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Color3.fromRGB(200, 190, 180)), Rotation = 90}, card)
	-- логотип Claude из 12 лучей, медленно вращается
	L.logo = make("Frame", {AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(52, 54), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(52, 52), BackgroundTransparency = 1}, card)
	local glow = make("Frame", {AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(70, 70), BackgroundColor3 = C.accent,
		BackgroundTransparency = 0.9, BorderSizePixel = 0}, L.logo)
	round(glow, 40)
	L.glow = glow
	local LEN = {10.2, 7.6, 10.8, 8.4, 9.9, 7.3, 10.9, 8.6, 9.6, 7.8, 10.5, 8.2}
	for i = 1, 12 do
		local a = math.rad(i * 30 - 90 + ((i % 2 == 0) and 3 or -2))
		local len = LEN[i] / 24 * 52
		local c = 52 / 24 * 1.2 + len / 2
		local r = make("Frame", {AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, math.cos(a) * c, 0.5, math.sin(a) * c), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(len, 4.5),
			Rotation = math.deg(a), BackgroundColor3 = C.accent, BorderSizePixel = 0}, L.logo)
		round(r, 3)
	end
	make("TextLabel", {Position = UDim2.fromOffset(92, 30), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(240, 30), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "Claude Client", Font = Enum.Font.Merriweather,
		TextSize = 26, TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(245, 240, 232), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left}, card)
	local ver = make("TextLabel", {Position = UDim2.fromOffset(92, 62), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(46, 18), BackgroundColor3 = C.accent, Text = UIK.VERSION, Font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono,
		TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BorderSizePixel = 0}, card)
	round(ver, 9)
	make("TextLabel", {Position = UDim2.fromOffset(146, 62), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(240, 18), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "ftap · " .. player.DisplayName, Font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono,
		TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = C.dim, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left}, card)
	L.stage = make("TextLabel", {Position = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 112), Size = UDim2.new(1, -110, 0, 18), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "Загрузка модулей…", Font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono,
		TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = C.dim, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left}, card)
	L.pct = make("TextLabel", {AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 112), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(70, 18), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "0%",
		Font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono, TextSize = 14, TextColor3 = C.accent, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right}, card)
	local track = make("Frame", {Position = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 138), Size = UDim2.new(1, -56, 0, 10), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(52, 48, 44), BorderSizePixel = 0, ClipsDescendants = true}, card)
	round(track, 5)
	L.fill = make("Frame", {Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1), BackgroundColor3 = C.accent, BorderSizePixel = 0}, track)
	round(L.fill, 5)
	make("UIGradient", {Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(226, 128, 92), C.accent)}, L.fill)
	L.shine = make("Frame", {Size = UDim2.new(0, 60, 1, 0), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 0.55, BorderSizePixel = 0}, L.fill)
	make("UIGradient", {Transparency = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0.2), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)})}, L.shine)
	make("TextLabel", {Position = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 160), Size = UDim2.new(1, -56, 0, 16), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "RightShift — меню  ·  /help — помощь",
		Font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono, TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = C.iconDim, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left}, card)
	-- плавное появление
	TweenService:Create(back, TweenInfo.new(0.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), {GroupTransparency = 0}):Play()
	TweenService:Create(holder, TweenInfo.new(0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Back), {Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)}):Play()
	local t0 = os.clock()
	L.conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
		local t = os.clock() - t0
		L.logo.Rotation = (t * 40) % 360
		L.glow.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(70 + math.sin(t * 3) * 6, 70 + math.sin(t * 3) * 6)
		L.shown += (L.p - L.shown) * math.min(dt * 8, 1)
		L.pct.Text = math.floor(L.shown * 100 + 0.5) .. "%"
		L.shine.Position = UDim2.new((t * 0.8) % 1.4 - 0.2, -30, 0, 0)
	end)
	function UIK.load(p, text) -- обновить прогресс и дать экрану перерисоваться
		if not L.gui.Parent then return end
		L.p = math.max(L.p, p)
		if text then L.stage.Text = text end
		TweenService:Create(L.fill, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart), {Size = UDim2.fromScale(L.p, 1)}):Play()
		task.wait()
	end
	function UIK.loadDone(nextStep) -- 100%, «Готово», плавный уход и дальше — интро или меню
		L.p = 1
		L.stage.Text = "Готово!"
		L.stage.TextColor3 = C.accent
		TweenService:Create(L.fill, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart), {Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)}):Play()
		task.delay(0.45, function()
			TweenService:Create(L.back, TweenInfo.new(0.45, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), {GroupTransparency = 1}):Play()
			TweenService:Create(L.scale, TweenInfo.new(0.45, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), {Scale = L.scale.Scale * 1.06}):Play()
			pcall(nextStep)
			task.delay(0.5, function()
				pcall(function() L.conn:Disconnect() end)
				pcall(function() L.gui:Destroy() end)
			end)
		end)
	end
end
UIK.load(0.05, "Загрузка модулей…")
local function setBase(b, color)
	b:SetAttribute("Base", color)
	tween(b, {BackgroundColor3 = color, TextColor3 = UIK.txt(color)}, 0.15)
end
local function attachHover(b)
	b.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
		local base = b:GetAttribute("Base")
		if base then tween(b, {BackgroundColor3 = base:Lerp((UIK.lum(base) > 0.5) and Color3.new(0, 0, 0) or Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 0.1)}, 0.1) end
	end)
	b.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
		local base = b:GetAttribute("Base")
		if base then tween(b, {BackgroundColor3 = base}, 0.1) end
	end)
end
local function button(parent, text, color, order, height)
	local b = make("TextButton", {
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, (height or 30) + UIK.thv), BackgroundColor3 = color, BackgroundTransparency = 0.35, AutoButtonColor = false,
		Text = text, TextColor3 = UIK.txt(color), Font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono, TextSize = 12, LayoutOrder = order or 0, BorderSizePixel = 0,
	}, parent)
	round(b, 7)
	local st = stroke(b, C.line, 1, 0.9)
	make("UIGradient", {Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Color3.fromRGB(234, 232, 228)), Rotation = 90}, b) -- лёгкий объём сверху вниз
	b:SetAttribute("Base", color)
	attachHover(b)
	b.MouseEnter:Connect(function() tween(st, {Color = C.accent, Transparency = 0.55}, 0.12) end) -- обводка подсвечивается акцентом
	b.MouseLeave:Connect(function() tween(st, {Color = C.line, Transparency = 0.9}, 0.12) end)
	local press = make("UIScale", {Scale = 1}, b)
	b.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function() tween(press, {Scale = 0.97}, 0.08) end)
	b.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function() tween(press, {Scale = 1}, 0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Back) end)
	b.MouseLeave:Connect(function() tween(press, {Scale = 1}, 0.1) end)
	return b
end

-- ======================= ИКОНКИ: белые, линейные, минималистичные =======================
-- Рисуются тонкими линиями и кругами на сетке 24×24, поэтому не нужны ни эмодзи, ни картинки из каталога.
UIK.ICONS = {}
local function Icon(name, parent, size, color, props)
	size = size or 18
	local f = make("Frame", {Name = "Icon", Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size), BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0}, parent)
	local th = math.max(1, size / 13)
	local P = {}
	local col = color or C.icon
	local D = {}
	D.w = th
	function D.l(x1, y1, x2, y2, w) -- линия со скруглёнными концами (w — своя толщина)
		local dx, dy = x2 - x1, y2 - y1
		local len = math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy)
		local l = make("Frame", {AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale((x1 + x2) / 48, (y1 + y2) / 48),
			Size = UDim2.new(len / 24, w or th, 0, w or th), Rotation = math.deg(math.atan2(dy, dx)), BackgroundColor3 = col, BorderSizePixel = 0}, f)
		make("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)}, l)
		P[#P + 1] = l
	end
	function D.e(cx, cy, rx, ry, fill, rot) -- эллипс / круг
		local e = make("Frame", {AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(cx / 24, cy / 24), Size = UDim2.fromScale(rx / 12, ry / 12),
			Rotation = rot or 0, BackgroundColor3 = col, BackgroundTransparency = fill and 0 or 1, BorderSizePixel = 0}, f)
		make("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)}, e)
		if fill then P[#P + 1] = e else P[#P + 1] = make("UIStroke", {Color = col, Thickness = th}, e) end
	end
	function D.c(cx, cy, r, fill) D.e(cx, cy, r, r, fill) end
	function D.r(x, y, w, h, rad, fill) -- прямоугольник со скруглением
		local e = make("Frame", {Position = UDim2.fromScale(x / 24, y / 24), Size = UDim2.fromScale(w / 24, h / 24), BackgroundColor3 = col,
			BackgroundTransparency = fill and 0 or 1, BorderSizePixel = 0}, f)
		make("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, (rad or 0) * size / 24)}, e)
		if fill then P[#P + 1] = e else P[#P + 1] = make("UIStroke", {Color = col, Thickness = th}, e) end
	end
	function D.arc(cx, cy, r, a0, a1, seg) -- дуга из коротких отрезков (градусы, 0 = вправо, 90 = вниз)
		seg = seg or 10
		local px, py
		for i = 0, seg do
			local a = math.rad(a0 + (a1 - a0) * i / seg)
			local x, y = cx + math.cos(a) * r, cy + math.sin(a) * r
			if px then D.l(px, py, x, y) end
			px, py = x, y
		end
	end
	function D.poly(pts, closed)
		for i = 1, #pts - (closed and 0 or 1) do
			local a, b = pts[i], pts[i % #pts + 1]
			D.l(a[1], a[2], b[1], b[2])
		end
	end
	;(UIK.ICONS[name] or UIK.ICONS.dot)(D, f)
	local obj = {frame = f, name = name}
	function obj.SetColor(c, t)
		for _, p in ipairs(P) do
			local key = p:IsA("UIStroke") and "Color" or "BackgroundColor3"
			if t then tween(p, {[key] = c}, t) else p[key] = c end
		end
	end
	for k, v in pairs(props or {}) do f[k] = v end
	return obj
end
function UIK.ICONS._star5(D, cx, cy, ro, ri)
	local pts = {}
	for i = 0, 9 do
		local a = math.rad(-90 + i * 36)
		local r = (i % 2 == 0) and ro or ri
		pts[#pts + 1] = {cx + math.cos(a) * r, cy + math.sin(a) * r}
	end
	D.poly(pts, true)
end
UIK.ICONS.dot = function(D) D.c(12, 12, 2.5, true) end
UIK.ICONS.hexagram = function(D) -- логотип Claude: «звёздочка» из 12 неровных лучей
	local L = {10.2, 7.6, 10.8, 8.4, 9.9, 7.3, 10.9, 8.6, 9.6, 7.8, 10.5, 8.2}
	for i = 1, 12 do
		local a = math.rad(i * 30 - 90 + ((i % 2 == 0) and 3 or -2))
		D.l(12 + math.cos(a) * 1.2, 12 + math.sin(a) * 1.2, 12 + math.cos(a) * L[i], 12 + math.sin(a) * L[i], D.w * 1.15)
	end
end
UIK.ICONS.claude = UIK.ICONS.hexagram
UIK.ICONS.drone = function(D) -- квадрокоптер сверху: X-рама, 4 винта, корпус и «искра» Claude в центре
	D.l(7, 7, 17, 17)
	D.l(17, 7, 7, 17)
	D.c(5.5, 5.5, 3.6)
	D.c(18.5, 5.5, 3.6)
	D.c(5.5, 18.5, 3.6)
	D.c(18.5, 18.5, 3.6)
	D.c(12, 12, 2.6, true)
end
UIK.ICONS.rocket = function(D, f)
	f.Rotation = 45
	D.r(8.5, 2.5, 7, 15.5, 3.5)
	D.c(12, 8, 1.7)
	D.poly({{8.5, 11.5}, {4.5, 17.5}, {8.5, 16.5}})
	D.poly({{15.5, 11.5}, {19.5, 17.5}, {15.5, 16.5}})
	D.l(10.5, 20, 10.5, 22.5)
	D.l(13.5, 20, 13.5, 22.5)
end
UIK.ICONS.paw = function(D)
	D.e(12, 16, 5.4, 4.4)
	D.c(4.6, 10, 2.3)
	D.c(9.2, 5, 2.3)
	D.c(14.8, 5, 2.3)
	D.c(19.4, 10, 2.3)
end
UIK.ICONS.spark = function(D)
	D.l(12, 2.5, 12, 21.5)
	D.l(2.5, 12, 21.5, 12)
	D.l(8, 8, 16, 16)
	D.l(16, 8, 8, 16)
end
UIK.ICONS.globe = function(D)
	D.c(12, 12, 9)
	D.e(12, 12, 3.8, 9)
	D.l(3, 12, 21, 12)
end
UIK.ICONS.user = function(D)
	D.c(12, 8, 3.6)
	D.arc(12, 21, 7.5, 180, 360, 10)
end
UIK.ICONS.users = function(D)
	D.c(9, 8.5, 3.2)
	D.arc(9, 21, 6.5, 180, 360, 9)
	D.arc(16, 8.5, 2.8, -100, 120, 6)
	D.arc(16, 20.5, 5.5, 230, 360, 6)
end
UIK.ICONS.gear = function(D)
	D.c(12, 12, 3)
	D.c(12, 12, 6.8)
	for i = 0, 7 do
		local a = math.rad(i * 45)
		D.l(12 + math.cos(a) * 6.8, 12 + math.sin(a) * 6.8, 12 + math.cos(a) * 9.6, 12 + math.sin(a) * 9.6)
	end
end
UIK.ICONS.power = function(D)
	D.l(12, 3, 12, 11)
	D.arc(12, 13, 8, -55, 235, 14)
end
UIK.ICONS.keyboard = function(D)
	D.r(2.5, 6, 19, 12.5, 2)
	for i = 0, 3 do D.c(6.5 + i * 3.7, 10, 0.7, true) end
	D.l(8, 14.5, 16, 14.5)
end
UIK.ICONS.search = function(D)
	D.c(10.5, 10.5, 6.5)
	D.l(15.3, 15.3, 20.5, 20.5)
end
UIK.ICONS.close = function(D)
	D.l(6, 6, 18, 18)
	D.l(18, 6, 6, 18)

local function ulower(s) -- нижний регистр с кириллицей
	local out = {}
	for _, c in utf8.codes(s) do
		if c >= 0x410 and c <= 0x42F then c += 32 elseif c == 0x401 then c = 0x451 end
		out[#out + 1] = utf8.char(c)
	end
	return table.concat(out):lower()
end

local conns = {}
local function connect(sig, fn)
	local c = sig:Connect(fn)
	conns[#conns + 1] = c
	return c
end

local function flatten(v)
	local f = Vector3.new(v.X, 0, v.Z)
	if f.Magnitude < 0.001 then return Vector3.new(0, 0, -1) end
	return f.Unit
end
local function safeUnit(v, fallback)
	if v.Magnitude < 1e-5 then return fallback end
	return v.Unit
end
local function easeOutCubic(x)
	x = math.clamp(x, 0, 1)
	return 1 - (1 - x) ^ 3
end
local function resolvePart(obj)
	if obj:IsA("BasePart") then return obj end
	return obj.PrimaryPart or obj:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
end
local function getHRP()
	local c = player.Character
	return c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
end
local function getFolder()
	return workspace:FindFirstChild(player.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
end
local function randomDir()
	local u, v = math.random() * 2 - 1, math.random() * TAU
	local s = math.sqrt(1 - u * u)
	return Vector3.new(s * math.cos(v), u, s * math.sin(v))
end
local function moveObj(obj, part, cf)
	if obj:IsA("Model") then obj:PivotTo(cf) else part.CFrame = cf end
end

local notify = function() end -- подменяется после создания интерфейса

-- ============================ ФИЗИКА И ЭФФЕКТЫ РАКЕТ (v8) ============================
-- Физика: у каждой ракеты своя скорость с инерцией. Двигатель тянет с ограниченным ускорением,
-- поэтому ракета разгоняется, закладывает дуги, слегка проскакивает цель и кренится в поворотах.
-- Эффекты: ядро пламени (синее на форсаже), языки огня, дым, искры, свет из сопла, шлейф,
-- конус пара и ударные кольца «звукового барьера» на большой скорости.
UIK.rfx = {on = true, smoke = true, inertia = true, cam = true, booms = true, man = {}, shakeUntil = 0}
function UIK.rfx.ns(...)
	local k = {}
	for i, v in ipairs({...}) do k[i] = NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(v[1], v[2]) end
	return NumberSequence.new(k)
end
UIK.rfx.warm = ColorSequence.new({ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 235)), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.35, Color3.fromRGB(255, 215, 110)),
	ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(255, 110, 20))})
UIK.rfx.hot = ColorSequence.new({ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(235, 245, 255)), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.35, Color3.fromRGB(120, 175, 255)),
	ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(255, 140, 60))})

-- st — таблица состояния ракеты, want — желаемая скорость, acc — тяга (макс. ускорение, студ/с²)
function UIK.rfx.drive(st, part, want, acc)
	local now = os.clock()
	local dt = math.clamp(now - (st.rlt or now - 1 / 60), 1 / 240, 1 / 20)
	st.rlt = now
	if not UIK.rfx.inertia then
		st.rvel, st.racc = want, Vector3.zero
		return want
	end
	local v = st.rvel or part.AssemblyLinearVelocity
	local dv = want - v
	local m = dv.Magnitude
	local lim = acc * dt
	if m > lim then dv = dv * (lim / m) end
	st.racc = (st.racc or Vector3.zero):Lerp(dv / dt, 0.3) -- сглаженное ускорение: для крена и пламени
	st.rvel = v + dv
	return st.rvel
end
-- крен по боковому ускорению: в повороте ракета ложится на бок, как самолёт
function UIK.rfx.roll(st)
	local target = 0
	if st.racc and st.rvel and st.rvel.Magnitude > 6 then
		local rt = st.rvel:Cross(Vector3.yAxis)
		if rt.Magnitude > 0.1 then target = math.clamp(st.racc:Dot(rt.Unit) / 140, -1.25, 1.25) end
	end
	st.rroll = (st.rroll or 0) + (target - (st.rroll or 0)) * 0.12
	return st.rroll
end
function UIK.rfx.throttle(st)
	local sp = st.rvel and st.rvel.Magnitude or 0
	local a = st.racc and st.racc.Magnitude or 0
	return math.clamp(0.06 + a / 700 + sp / 380, 0.06, 1)
end
function UIK.rfx.clear(st)
	local f = st.rfx
	if not f then return end
	for _, o in ipairs({f.att, f.mid, f.a0, f.a1, f.trail}) do pcall(function() o:Destroy() end) end
	st.rfx = nil
end
function UIK.rfx.build(st, part)
	UIK.rfx.clear(st)
	local h = part.Size.Y / 2 -- нос = +Y, сопло = -Y
	local w = math.clamp(math.min(part.Size.X, part.Size.Z) * 0.5, 0.3, 2)
	local f = {}
	f.att = make("Attachment", {Name = "FembiixNoz", Position = Vector3.new(0, -h, 0)}, part)
	f.mid = make("Attachment", {Name = "FembiixMid"}, part)
	local function pe(at, props)
		props.Rate = 0
		props.EmissionDirection = props.EmissionDirection or Enum.NormalId.Bottom
		return make("ParticleEmitter", props, at)
	end
	f.core = pe(f.att, {LightEmission = 1, LightInfluence = 0, ZOffset = 1, Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.06, 0.16), Speed = NumberRange.new(18, 30),
		SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(3, 3), Size = UIK.rfx.ns({0, w * 1.3}, {1, w * 0.2}), Transparency = UIK.rfx.ns({0, 0}, {0.6, 0.3}, {1, 1}), Color = UIK.rfx.warm})
	f.flame = pe(f.att, {LightEmission = 1, LightInfluence = 0, Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.18, 0.4), Speed = NumberRange.new(10, 20), SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(12, 12),
		Size = UIK.rfx.ns({0, w * 1.6}, {0.4, w * 2}, {1, w * 0.3}), Transparency = UIK.rfx.ns({0, 0.15}, {0.7, 0.5}, {1, 1}), Rotation = NumberRange.new(0, 360), RotSpeed = NumberRange.new(-200, 200),
		Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(255, 170, 50), Color3.fromRGB(200, 40, 10))})
	f.smoke = pe(f.att, {LightEmission = 0, LightInfluence = 1, Lifetime = NumberRange.new(1.2, 2.4), Speed = NumberRange.new(2, 5), SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(22, 22), Drag = 2,
		Size = UIK.rfx.ns({0, w * 1.2}, {1, w * 5}), Transparency = UIK.rfx.ns({0, 0.6}, {0.3, 0.7}, {1, 1}), Acceleration = Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0),
		Rotation = NumberRange.new(0, 360), RotSpeed = NumberRange.new(-40, 40), Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(215, 210, 205), Color3.fromRGB(110, 108, 106))})
	f.sparks = pe(f.att, {LightEmission = 1, LightInfluence = 0, Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.3, 0.7), Speed = NumberRange.new(15, 35), SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(28, 28), Drag = 3,
		Size = UIK.rfx.ns({0, 0.18}, {1, 0}), Acceleration = Vector3.new(0, -40, 0), Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(255, 240, 160), Color3.fromRGB(255, 120, 30))})
	f.vapor = pe(f.mid, {LightEmission = 0.3, LightInfluence = 0.6, Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.12, 0.28), Speed = NumberRange.new(0, 1), SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(180, 180),
		Size = UIK.rfx.ns({0, w * 2.5}, {1, w * 5}), Transparency = UIK.rfx.ns({0, 0.65}, {1, 1}), Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(240, 245, 255))})
	f.light = make("PointLight", {Range = 10, Brightness = 0, Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 160, 60), Shadows = false}, f.att)
	f.a0 = make("Attachment", {Name = "FembiixT0", Position = Vector3.new(w, -h * 0.7, 0)}, part)
	f.a1 = make("Attachment", {Name = "FembiixT1", Position = Vector3.new(-w, -h * 0.7, 0)}, part)
	f.trail = make("Trail", {Attachment0 = f.a0, Attachment1 = f.a1, Lifetime = 0.45, MinLength = 0.1, FaceCamera = true, LightEmission = 0.25, Enabled = false,
		Transparency = UIK.rfx.ns({0, 0.45}, {1, 1}), WidthScale = UIK.rfx.ns({0, 1}, {1, 0.15}), Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(255, 250, 240), C.dim)}, part)
	f.hot = false
	st.rfx = f
end
-- ударная волна «звукового барьера»: два кольца расходятся, рядом с камерой — лёгкая тряска
function UIK.rfx.boom(pos, dir)
	for i = 1, 2 do
		local p = pos - dir * (i * 2)
		local ring = make("Part", {Name = "FembiixBoom", Shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder, Anchored = true, CanCollide = false, CanQuery = false, CanTouch = false, CastShadow = false,
			Material = Enum.Material.ForceField, Color = Color3.fromRGB(220, 235, 255), Size = Vector3.new(0.3, 3, 3),
			CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(p, p + dir) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.pi / 2, 0)}, camera)
		tween(ring, {Size = Vector3.new(0.3, 30 + i * 12, 30 + i * 12), Transparency = 1}, 0.5 + i * 0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
		task.delay(0.75 + i * 0.15, function() ring:Destroy() end)
	end
	if (camera.CFrame.Position - pos).Magnitude < 90 then UIK.rfx.shakeUntil = os.clock() + 0.3 end
end
function UIK.rfx.update(st, part, hidden)
	if not UIK.rfx.on or hidden then
		UIK.rfx.clear(st)
		return
	end
	if not st.rfx or not st.rfx.att.Parent then UIK.rfx.build(st, part) end
	local f = st.rfx
	local vel = st.rvel or part.AssemblyLinearVelocity
	local sp = vel.Magnitude
	local t = UIK.rfx.throttle(st)
	f.core.Rate = 35 + 280 * t
	f.flame.Rate = 20 + 150 * t
	f.smoke.Rate = UIK.rfx.smoke and (4 + 45 * t) or 0
	f.sparks.Rate = (t > 0.55) and 35 * t or 0
	f.vapor.Rate = (sp > 220) and math.min((sp - 220) * 0.6, 80) or 0
	f.light.Brightness = (1.2 + 3.5 * t) * (0.8 + math.random() * 0.4) -- живое мерцание
	f.light.Range = 8 + 12 * t
	f.trail.Enabled = sp > 55
	local hot = t > 0.78 -- форсаж: ядро пламени синеет
	if hot ~= f.hot then
		f.hot = hot
		f.core.Color = hot and UIK.rfx.hot or UIK.rfx.warm
		f.light.Color = hot and Color3.fromRGB(170, 200, 255) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 160, 60)
	end
	if UIK.rfx.booms then
		if sp > 260 and not st.rboom then
			st.rboom = true
			UIK.rfx.boom(part.Position, vel.Unit)
		elseif sp < 170 then
			st.rboom = false
		end
	end
end
