	[0x41E] = "O", [0x420] = "P", [0x421] = "C", [0x422] = "T", [0x423] = "Y", [0x425] = "X"}) do
	TXT.CYR[utf8.char(cyr)] = lat
end
TXT.G[utf8.char(0x411)] = {{{2,4},{0,4},{0,0},{1.4,0},{2,0.9},{1.4,2.1},{0,2.1}}} -- Б
TXT.G[utf8.char(0x413)] = {{{0,0},{0,4},{2,4}}} -- Г
TXT.G[utf8.char(0x418)] = {{{0,4},{0,0},{2,4},{2,0}}} -- И
TXT.G[utf8.char(0x41B)] = {{{0,0},{0.6,4},{2,4},{2,0}}} -- Л
TXT.G[utf8.char(0x41F)] = {{{0,0},{0,4},{2,4},{2,0}}} -- П
TXT.G[utf8.char(0x424)] = {{{1,0},{1,4}}, {{1,3.1},{0.3,2.9},{0,2},{0.3,1.1},{1,0.9},{1.7,1.1},{2,2},{1.7,2.9},{1,3.1}}} -- Ф

function TXT.build(text)
	local chars = {}
	for _, cp in utf8.codes(text) do
		if cp >= 0x430 and cp <= 0x44F then cp -= 32 end
		local ch = utf8.char(cp):upper()
		chars[#chars + 1] = TXT.CYR[ch] or ch
		if #chars >= 14 then break end
	end
	local n = #chars
	local gap = 1.1
	local totalW = n * 2 + math.max(n - 1, 0) * gap
	local segs, C0 = {}, 0
	local pen, first
	local function add(a, b, down)
		local len = (b - a).Magnitude
		if len < 1e-4 then return end
		local cost = down and len or len * 0.4 -- пустой перелёт быстрее
		segs[#segs + 1] = {a = a, b = b, down = down, cost = cost, cum = C0}
		C0 += cost
	end
	for ci, ch in ipairs(chars) do
		local g = TXT.G[ch]
		if g then
			local ox = (ci - 1) * (2 + gap) - totalW / 2
			for _, stroke in ipairs(g) do
				local p0 = Vector2.new(ox + stroke[1][1], stroke[1][2] - 2)
				if pen then add(pen, p0, false) else first = p0 end
				pen = p0
				for k = 2, #stroke do
					local p = Vector2.new(ox + stroke[k][1], stroke[k][2] - 2)
					add(pen, p, true)
					pen = p
				end
			end
		end
	end
	if pen and first then add(pen, first, false) end
	return {segs = segs, cost = C0, totalW = totalW}
end

function TXT.penAt(path, s)
	local segs = path.segs
	local lo, hi = 1, #segs
	while lo < hi do
		local mid = math.floor((lo + hi + 1) / 2)
		if segs[mid].cum <= s then lo = mid else hi = mid - 1 end
	end
	local sg = segs[lo]
	local f = math.clamp((s - sg.cum) / sg.cost, 0, 1)
	return sg.a:Lerp(sg.b, f), sg.down
end

function TXT.get()
	if TXT.key ~= TXT.text then
		TXT.path = TXT.build(TXT.text)
		TXT.key = TXT.text
	end
	return TXT.path
end

-- настройка шлейфа под режим надписи (тонкий и долгий, чтобы слово было видно целиком)
function TXT.fx(s, on, life)
	if on then
		if s.tr and (s.textFx ~= true or math.abs((s.trLife or 0) - life) > 0.05) then
			s.tr.Lifetime = life
			s.trLife = life
			if s.textFx ~= true then
				s.tr.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0, 0.85)
				if s.a0 then s.a0.Position = Vector3.new(0, 0.22, 0) end
				if s.a1 then s.a1.Position = Vector3.new(0, -0.22, 0) end
			end
		end
		s.textFx = true
	elseif s.textFx then
		s.textFx = nil
		s.ap.Enabled = true
		if s.tr then
			s.tr.Enabled = true
			s.tr.Lifetime = 0.7
			s.tr.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0.1, 1)
		end
		if s.a0 then s.a0.Position = Vector3.new(0, 0.4, 0) end
		if s.a1 then s.a1.Position = Vector3.new(0, -0.4, 0) end
		if s.pe then s.pe.Enabled = true end
	end
end

local function updateSparklers(dt, now)
	wire.used = 0
	local hrp = getHRP()
	if not hrp then wireEnd(); bhHide(); return end

	for i = #sparklers, 1, -1 do
		local s = sparklers[i]
		if not s.part.Parent or not s.part:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then releaseSparklerAt(i) end
	end
	local maxN = ui.spCount.Get()
	while #sparklers > maxN do releaseSparklerAt(#sparklers) end

	captureTimer -= dt
	if autoCapture and captureTimer <= 0 and #sparklers < maxN then
		captureTimer = 0.5
		captureNearby()
	end

	local N = #sparklers
	if ui.spStatus then
		local txt = ("Захвачено: %d / %d"):format(N, maxN)
		if ui.spStatus.Text ~= txt then ui.spStatus.Text = txt end
	end
	if N == 0 then bhHide(); wireEnd(); return end

	if sparkMorph then -- смена режима: плавный перелёт в новую фигуру
		sparkMorph = false
		for _, s in ipairs(sparklers) do
			s.prev = s.part.Position
			s.m0 = now
		end
	end

	local R0, H, W, resp = ui.spRadius.Get(), ui.spHeight.Get(), ui.spSpin.Get(), ui.spResp.Get()
	local R = R0 * (1 + 0.05 * math.sin(now * 3)) -- «дыхание» фигуры
	local flat = flatten(hrp.CFrame.LookVector)
	baseLook = safeUnit(baseLook:Lerp(flat, math.clamp(dt * 10, 0, 1)), flat)
	local base = CFrame.lookAt(hrp.Position, hrp.Position + baseLook)
	local mode = sparkModeKey
	local jitter = (mode == "CUBE" or mode == "PENTAGRAM" or mode == "NAME" or mode == "PYRAMID" or mode == "CROWN" or mode == "STAR8" or mode == "NEWTON" or mode == "LIGHTNING" or mode == "LIGHTHOUSE" or mode == "SCANNER") and 0 or 0.4

	-- режим «Надпись»: огни — это перья, они очень быстро обводят буквы
	local textMode, path, unit, vGrid, dist, cy, trailLife = false, nil, 1, 1, 14, 0, 1
	if mode == "NAME" then
		path = TXT.get()
		if path and #path.segs > 0 and path.cost > 0 then
			textMode = true
			local hGlyph = math.max(R0, 2) * 0.6
			unit = hGlyph / 4
			vGrid = (14 + W * 9) / unit
			dist = math.max(14, path.totalW * unit * 0.8)
			cy = H + hGlyph * 0.5 + 3
			trailLife = math.clamp(path.cost / (N * vGrid) * 1.1 + 0.15, 0.3, 4)
		end
	end

	local chain
	if mode == "TAIL" then
		local back = -baseLook
		local spacing = math.max(1.5, R * 0.35)
		chain = tail
		chain[1] = hrp.Position + Vector3.new(0, H * 0.3, 0) + back * 2.2
		for i = 2, N do
			local prev = chain[i - 1]
			local cur = chain[i] or (prev + back * spacing)
			local d = cur - prev
			if d.Magnitude < 0.01 then d = back end
			chain[i] = prev + d.Unit * spacing
		end
	end
	local right = baseLook:Cross(Vector3.yAxis)

	for i, s in ipairs(sparklers) do
		local target, penDown
		if textMode then
			local sp = (now * vGrid + (i - 1) * path.cost / N) % path.cost
			local p2, down = TXT.penAt(path, sp)
			target = base:PointToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(p2.X * unit, cy + p2.Y * unit, -dist))
			penDown = down
		elseif chain then
			target = chain[i] + right * (math.sin(now * W * 2 - i * 0.7) * 0.8)
		else
			local dp = (mode == "DRAGON" and API.dragonSparkPos) and API.dragonSparkPos(i, N, now) or nil
			if dp then
				target = dp
			else
				target = base:PointToWorldSpace(sparkOffset((mode == "DRAGON") and "COMET" or mode, i, N, now, R, H, W))
			end
		end
		if jitter > 0 then
			target += Vector3.new(math.noise(now * 1.3, i * 7.1, 0), math.noise(now * 1.1, i * 3.3, 5), math.noise(now * 1.2, i * 5.5, 9)) * jitter
		end
		local m = easeOutCubic((now - s.m0) / 0.9)
		if m < 1 then
			target = s.prev:Lerp(target, m)
			penDown = false
		end

		if textMode then
			TXT.fx(s, true, trailLife)
			s.ap.Enabled = false -- перо двигаем напрямую, без задержки физики
			moveObj(s.obj, s.part, CFrame.new(target))
			s.part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
			s.part.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
			if s.tr then s.tr.Enabled = penDown and true or false end
			if s.pe then s.pe.Enabled = penDown and true or false end
		else
			TXT.fx(s, false)
			s.ap.Position = target
			s.ap.Responsiveness = resp
		end
		s.ao.CFrame = CFrame.Angles(0, now * 2 + i, 0)

		-- эффекты: чем быстрее огонь, тем гуще искры; свет мерцает
		local vel = textMode and 90 or s.part.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude
		if s.pe then s.pe.Rate = math.clamp(55 + vel * 3.5, 55, 240) end
		if s.pl then s.pl.Brightness = 1.1 + math.random() * 1.4 end
		if sparkRainbow then
			local h = (now * 0.12 + i / N) % 1
			paintSparkler(s, Color3.fromHSV(h, 0.65, 1), Color3.fromHSV((h + 0.1) % 1, 1, 1))
			s.rb = true
		elseif s.rb ~= false then
			paintSparkler(s, SPARK_C1, SPARK_C2)
			s.rb = false
		end
	end

	-- каркасы
	if mode == "CUBE" then
		local rot = cubeBasis(now, W)
		local col = wireColor(now)
		local corners = {}
		for ci = 0, 7 do corners[ci] = base:PointToWorldSpace(cubeCorner(ci, rot, R, H)) end
		local th = 0.14 + 0.04 * math.sin(now * 9)
		for ci = 0, 7 do
			for _, bit in ipairs({1, 2, 4}) do
				local cj = bit32.bxor(ci, bit)
				if cj > ci then wireLine(corners[ci], corners[cj], col, th, true) end
			end
		end
	elseif mode == "PENTAGRAM" then
		local col = wireColor(now)
		for j = 0, 4 do
			wireLine(base:PointToWorldSpace(starVertex(j, now, R, H)), base:PointToWorldSpace(starVertex(j + 1, now, R, H)), col, 0.13, true)
		end
		local y = -2.2 + H * 0.25
		for j = 0, 19 do
			local a1, a2 = j / 20 * TAU + now * 0.4, (j + 1) / 20 * TAU + now * 0.4
			wireLine(
				base:PointToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(math.sin(a1) * R, y, -math.cos(a1) * R)),
				base:PointToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(math.sin(a2) * R, y, -math.cos(a2) * R)),
				col, 0.09, false)
		end
	end
	if mode == "PYRAMID" then
		local col = wireColor(now)
		local apex = base:PointToWorldSpace(TXT.pyrVert(0, now, R, H, W))
		local bs = {}
		for i = 1, 4 do bs[i] = base:PointToWorldSpace(TXT.pyrVert(i, now, R, H, W)) end
		for i = 1, 4 do
			wireLine(apex, bs[i], col, 0.13, true)
			wireLine(bs[i], bs[i % 4 + 1], col, 0.13, true)
		end
	elseif mode == "STAR8" then
		local col = wireColor(now)
		for i = 1, N do
			wireLine(base:PointToWorldSpace(sparkOffset("STAR8", i, N, now, R, H, W)), base:PointToWorldSpace(sparkOffset("STAR8", i % N + 1, N, now, R, H, W)), col, 0.1, true)
		end
	elseif mode == "LIGHTHOUSE" then
		local col = wireColor(now)
		local c = base:PointToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(0, H + 3, 0))
		for i = 1, N do wireLine(c, base:PointToWorldSpace(sparkOffset("LIGHTHOUSE", i, N, now, R, H, W)), col, 0.12, true) end
	elseif mode == "LIGHTNING" then
		local col = Color3.fromHSV(0.62, 0.35, 1)
		for i = 1, N - 1 do
			wireLine(base:PointToWorldSpace(sparkOffset("LIGHTNING", i, N, now, R, H, W)), base:PointToWorldSpace(sparkOffset("LIGHTNING", i + 1, N, now, R, H, W)), col, 0.14, true)
		end
	elseif mode == "NEWTON" then
		for i = 1, N do
			local x = (i - 1 - (N - 1) / 2) * 1.6
			wireLine(base:PointToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(x, H + R * 0.9 + 4, -R * 0.7)), base:PointToWorldSpace(sparkOffset("NEWTON", i, N, now, R, H, W)), Color3.fromRGB(200, 205, 225), 0.06, false)
		end
	elseif mode == "DRAGON" then -- языки пламени из пасти при дыхании
		local di = API.dragonInfo and API.dragonInfo()
		if di and di.breath > 0 then
			for k = 1, 10 do
				local w = now * 9 + k * 2.3
				local rnd = Vector3.new(math.noise(w, 0.3, 0), math.noise(w, 4.1, 0), math.noise(w, 8.9, 0))
				local tip = di.head + di.fwd * (6 + 14 * di.breath * (0.5 + math.random() * 0.5)) + rnd * 6 * di.breath
				wireLine(di.head + di.fwd * 1.5, tip, Color3.fromHSV(0.02 + math.random() * 0.1, 1, 1), 0.2 + math.random() * 0.25, true)
			end
		end
	elseif mode == "CROWN" then
		local col = wireColor(now)
		for i = 1, N do
			local a = base:PointToWorldSpace(sparkOffset("CROWN", i, N, now, R, H, W))
			local b = base:PointToWorldSpace(sparkOffset("CROWN", i % N + 1, N, now, R, H, W))
			wireLine(a, b, col, 0.1, true)
		end
	end
	wireEnd()

	if mode == "BLACKHOLE" then
		local c = base:PointToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(0, H + 2, -(R * 1.2 + 2)))
		local pulse = 1 + 0.08 * math.sin(now * 5)
		bhCore.Size = Vector3.new(2.4, 2.4, 2.4) * pulse
		bhGlow.Size = Vector3.new(3.6, 3.6, 3.6) * pulse
		bhCore.CFrame = CFrame.new(c)
		bhGlow.CFrame = CFrame.new(c)
		bhCore.Parent = camera
		bhGlow.Parent = camera
	else
		bhHide()
	end
end

UIK.toastN = 0
notify = function(text, color)
	UIK.toastN += 1
	local warn_ = (color == C.danger)
	local accent = C.accent
	local wrap = make("Frame", {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(322, 62), BackgroundTransparency = 1, LayoutOrder = UIK.toastN}, toastHolder)
	local cd = make("Frame", {Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(340, 0), BackgroundColor3 = C.bg, BackgroundTransparency = 0.12,
		BorderSizePixel = 0, ClipsDescendants = true}, wrap)
	round(cd, 10)
	stroke(cd, C.line, 1, 0.88)
	Icon(warn_ and "warn" or "claude", cd, 22, C.accent, {Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14)})
	make("TextLabel", {Size = UDim2.new(1, -56, 0, 16), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(46, 6), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "Claude", Font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono,
		TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = C.white, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left}, cd)
	make("TextLabel", {Size = UDim2.new(1, -56, 0, 32), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(46, 22), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = text, Font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono, TextSize = 11,
		TextColor3 = C.dim, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top, TextWrapped = true}, cd)
	local bar = make("Frame", {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 2), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -2), BackgroundColor3 = accent, BackgroundTransparency = 0.3, BorderSizePixel = 0}, cd)
	tween(cd, {Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)}, 0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
	tween(bar, {Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 3)}, 3, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear)
	local kids = {}
	for _, ch in ipairs(toastHolder:GetChildren()) do
		if ch:IsA("Frame") then kids[#kids + 1] = ch end
	end
	if #kids > 6 then
		table.sort(kids, function(a, b) return a.LayoutOrder < b.LayoutOrder end)
		kids[1]:Destroy()
	end
	task.delay(3.1, function()
		if not wrap.Parent then return end
		tween(cd, {Position = UDim2.fromOffset(340, 0)}, 0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
		task.delay(0.28, function() if wrap.Parent then wrap:Destroy() end end)
	end)
end

			TextColor3 = C.text, Font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono, TextSize = 12, TextWrapped = true, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
			BorderSizePixel = 0, Visible = false, ZIndex = 200}, screenGui)

		make("UIPadding", {PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 7), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 7), PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 10), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 10)}, tf)
		local cur, since, curText = nil, 0, ""
		function UIK.tip(obj, text)
			obj.MouseEnter:Connect(function() cur, since, curText = obj, os.clock(), text end)
			obj.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
				if cur == obj then cur = nil; tf.Visible = false end
			end)
		end
		connect(RunService.RenderStepped, function()
			if cur and os.clock() - since > 0.35 and cur.Parent then
				local m, vs = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation(), camera.ViewportSize
				tf.Text = curText
				tf.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(math.min(m.X + 16, vs.X - 262), math.min(m.Y + 18, vs.Y - 90))
				tf.Visible = true
			elseif tf.Visible and not cur then
				tf.Visible = false
			end
		end)
	end
end

local userMul = API.cfg.userMul or 1
local function autoScale()
	local vp = camera.ViewportSize
	return math.clamp(math.min((vp.X - 24) / UIK.W, (vp.Y - 24) / UIK.H), 0.45, 1.15)
end
local userScale = autoScale() * userMul

	Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Active = true}, screenGui)
local uiScale = make("UIScale", {Scale = userScale}, MainFrame)
connect(camera:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"), function()
	userScale = autoScale() * userMul
	uiScale.Scale = userScale
end)
MainFrame.MouseEnter:Connect(function() overMain = true end)
MainFrame.MouseLeave:Connect(function() overMain = false end)

		ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop}, Win)
	round(UIK.bg, 12)

	-- боковая панель как в Claude: логотип с названием, вкладки с подписями
	local SW = UIK.SW
	;(function()
		local side = make("Frame", {Size = UDim2.new(0, SW, 1, 0), BackgroundColor3 = C.panel, BackgroundTransparency = 0.3, BorderSizePixel = 0}, Win)
		make("Frame", {Size = UDim2.new(0, 1, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), BackgroundColor3 = C.line, BackgroundTransparency = 0.9, BorderSizePixel = 0}, side)
		UIK.logoIc = Icon("claude", side, 24, C.accent, {AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(25, 26)})
		make("TextLabel", {Size = UDim2.new(1, -48, 0, 16), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(44, 11), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "Claude Client", Font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono,
			TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = C.text, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left}, side)
		make("TextLabel", {Size = UDim2.new(1, -48, 0, 12), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(44, 28), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = UIK.VERSION .. " · ftap", Font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono,
			TextSize = 10, TextColor3 = C.dim, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left}, side)
		UIK.ind = make("Frame", {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 18), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 70), BackgroundColor3 = C.accent, BorderSizePixel = 0}, side)
		round(UIK.ind, 2)
		local function row(iconName, label, y, fromBottom)
			local b = make("TextButton", {Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 32 + UIK.thv), Position = fromBottom and UDim2.new(0, 8, 1, y) or UDim2.fromOffset(8, y),
				AnchorPoint = fromBottom and Vector2.new(0, 1) or Vector2.new(0, 0), BackgroundColor3 = C.accent, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "",
				AutoButtonColor = false, BorderSizePixel = 0}, side)
			round(b, 7)
			local ps = make("UIScale", {Scale = 1}, b) -- вкладка чуть проседает при нажатии
			b.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function() tween(ps, {Scale = 0.95}, 0.07) end)
			b.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function() tween(ps, {Scale = 1}, 0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Back) end)
			b.MouseLeave:Connect(function() tween(ps, {Scale = 1}, 0.15) end)
			local ic = Icon(iconName, b, 16, C.iconDim, {AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0.5, 0)})
			local lbl = make("TextLabel", {Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(34, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = label, Font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono,
				TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = C.dim, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd}, b)
			return b, ic, lbl
		end
		for i, tb in ipairs(TABS) do
			local y = 60 + (i - 1) * (36 + UIK.thv)
			local b, ic, lbl = row(tb[2], tb[3], y)
			b.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
				if currentTab ~= tb[1] then ic.SetColor(C.icon, 0.1); tween(lbl, {TextColor3 = C.text}, 0.1); tween(b, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.94}, 0.1) end
			end)
			b.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
				if currentTab ~= tb[1] then ic.SetColor(C.iconDim, 0.1); tween(lbl, {TextColor3 = C.dim}, 0.1); tween(b, {BackgroundTransparency = 1}, 0.1) end
			end)
			tabBtns[tb[1]] = {btn = b, y = y, icon = ic, lbl = lbl}
			b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() selectTab(tb[1]) end)
		end
		for _, it in ipairs({{"power", "Паника  End", -46, function() API.panic() end}, {"keyboard", "Клавиши  /help", -10, function() UIK.showHelp() end}}) do
			local b, ic, lbl = row(it[1], it[2], it[3], true)
			b.MouseEnter:Connect(function() ic.SetColor(C.accent, 0.1); tween(lbl, {TextColor3 = C.text}, 0.1) end)
			b.MouseLeave:Connect(function() ic.SetColor(C.iconDim, 0.1); tween(lbl, {TextColor3 = C.dim}, 0.1) end)
			b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(it[4])
		end
	end)()

	-- верхняя полоса: поиск во всю ширину + перетаскивание, свернуть, скрыть
	local top = make("Frame", {Size = UDim2.new(1, -SW, 0, 44), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(SW, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Active = true}, Win)
	local searchB = make("TextButton", {Size = UDim2.new(1, -110, 0, 28), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 9), BackgroundColor3 = C.panel, BackgroundTransparency = 0.4,
		Text = "      поиск режимов, неба, шляп…", Font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono, TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = C.dim, AutoButtonColor = false, BorderSizePixel = 0}, top)
	round(searchB, 14)
	stroke(searchB, C.line, 1, 0.9)
	Icon("search", searchB, 14, C.iconDim, {AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0.5, 0)})
	make("TextLabel", {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(60, 28), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "Ctrl+K",
		Font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono, TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = C.iconDim, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right}, searchB)
	searchB.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() UIK.openPalette() end)
	local function tbtn(iconName, x, tipText)
		local b = make("TextButton", {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 28), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, x, 0, 9), BackgroundColor3 = C.line,
			BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BorderSizePixel = 0}, top)
		round(b, 7)
		local ic = Icon(iconName, b, 16, C.iconDim, {AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)})
		UIK.tip(b, tipText)
		b.MouseEnter:Connect(function() ic.SetColor(C.white, 0.1); tween(b, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.93}, 0.1) end)
		b.MouseLeave:Connect(function() ic.SetColor(C.iconDim, 0.1); tween(b, {BackgroundTransparency = 1}, 0.1) end)
		return b
	end
	local closeB = tbtn("close", -8, "Скрыть окно (RightShift / H)")
	local minB = tbtn("minus", -38, "Свернуть")
	tbtn("move", -68, "Перетащи окно за верхнюю полосу")

	local dragging, dragStart, startPos
	if type(API.cfg.winPos) == "table" then MainFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, API.cfg.winPos[1] or 0, 0.5, API.cfg.winPos[2] or 0) end
	top.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			dragging, dragStart, startPos = true, input.Position, MainFrame.Position
			input.Changed:Connect(function()
				if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End and dragging then
					dragging = false
					API.cfg.winPos = {MainFrame.Position.X.Offset, MainFrame.Position.Y.Offset}
					API.save()
				end
			end)
		end
	end)
	connect(UserInputService.InputChanged, function(input)
		if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
			local d = (input.Position - dragStart) / uiScale.Scale
			MainFrame.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + d.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + d.Y)
		end
	end)

	pagesHolder = make("Frame", {Size = UDim2.new(1, -(SW + 20), 1, -(46 + 70 + UIK.thv)), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(SW + 10, 46), BackgroundTransparency = 1, ClipsDescendants = true}, Win)
	-- строка ввода как в Claude Code: «> » и команда / название режима, Enter — выполнить
	;(function()
		local bar = make("Frame", {Size = UDim2.new(1, -(SW + 20), 0, 34 + UIK.thv), Position = UDim2.new(0, SW + 10, 1, -60 - UIK.thv), BackgroundColor3 = C.bg,
			BackgroundTransparency = 0.05, BorderSizePixel = 0}, Win)
		round(bar, 8)
		UIK.promptStroke = stroke(bar, C.dim, 1, 0.55)
		make("TextLabel", {Size = UDim2.new(0, 22, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = ">", Font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono,
			TextSize = 15, TextColor3 = C.accent}, bar)
		local tb = make("TextBox", {Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(30, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", ClearTextOnFocus = false,
			PlaceholderText = 'Try "дракон"', PlaceholderColor3 = C.iconDim, TextColor3 = C.text, Font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono, TextSize = 13,
			TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left}, bar)
		UIK.prompt = tb
		tb.Focused:Connect(function() tween(UIK.promptStroke, {Color = C.accent, Transparency = 0.15}, 0.15) end)
		tb.FocusLost:Connect(function(enter)
			tween(UIK.promptStroke, {Color = C.dim, Transparency = 0.55}, 0.15)
			local q = tb.Text
			if enter and q:gsub("%s", "") ~= "" then
				tb.Text = ""
				if UIK.runPrompt then UIK.runPrompt(q) end
			end
		end)
		UIK.hintL = make("TextLabel", {Size = UDim2.new(0.6, 0, 0, 16), Position = UDim2.new(0, SW + 14, 1, -21), BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			Text = "? помощь  ·  / команда  ·  Ctrl+K поиск", TextColor3 = C.iconDim, Font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono, TextSize = 10, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left}, Win)
	end)()
	local footer = make("TextLabel", {Size = UDim2.new(1, -(SW + 24), 0, 16), Position = UDim2.new(0, SW + 10, 1, -21), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "",
		TextColor3 = C.iconDim, Font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono, TextSize = 10, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right}, Win)
	local fpsAcc, fpsN = 0, 0
	function ui.footerTick(dt)
		fpsAcc += dt
		fpsN += 1
		if fpsAcc >= 0.5 then
			local fps = math.floor(fpsN / fpsAcc + 0.5)
			fpsAcc, fpsN = 0, 0
			local ping = 0
			pcall(function() ping = math.floor(game:GetService("Stats").Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue()) end)
			ui.statusPill.Text = ("Claude Client %s  |  %d fps  |  %d ms"):format(UIK.VERSION, fps, ping)
			footer.Text = ("%s  ·  %d fps  ·  %d ms"):format(DEVICE, fps, ping)
			UIK.tipN = (UIK.tipN or 0) + 1
			if UIK.prompt and UIK.tipN % 8 == 0 then -- подсказки в строке ввода меняются, как в Claude Code
				local ex = {'Try "дракон"', 'Try "орбита"', 'Try "звезда"', 'Try "закат"', 'Try "корона"', 'Try "/panic"', 'Try "/help"', 'Try "бобик"'}
				UIK.prompt.PlaceholderText = ex[(UIK.tipN / 8) % #ex + 1]
			end
		end
		-- «работаю…»: пока ракеты или огни заняты, логотип крутится, как спиннер Claude Code
		local busy = #swarmRockets + #sparklers
		if UIK.logoIc then
			if busy > 0 then
				UIK.logoIc.frame.Rotation = (UIK.logoIc.frame.Rotation + dt * 60) % 360
			elseif UIK.logoIc.frame.Rotation ~= 0 then
				UIK.logoIc.frame.Rotation = 0
			end
		end
		if UIK.hintL then
			local txt = (busy > 0) and (("работаю" .. string.rep(".", math.floor(os.clock() * 2) % 3 + 1) .. "  ракет %d  ·  огней %d"):format(#swarmRockets, #sparklers))
				or "? помощь  ·  / команда  ·  Ctrl+K поиск"
			if UIK.hintL.Text ~= txt then
				UIK.hintL.Text = txt
				UIK.hintL.TextColor3 = (busy > 0) and C.accent or C.iconDim
			end
		end
	end
	function UIK.setCompact(on)
		UIK.compact = on and true or false
		API.cfg.compact = UIK.compact
		API.save()
		footer.Visible = not UIK.compact
	end
	if API.cfg.compact then UIK.setCompact(true) end
	function UIK.setBgT(v) UIK.bg.ImageTransparency = v; API.cfg.bgT = v; API.save() end
	function UIK.setWinT(v) UIK.win.BackgroundTransparency = v; API.cfg.winT = v; API.save() end

	local minimized = false
	minB.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		minimized = not minimized
		tween(Win, {Size = minimized and UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 46) or UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)}, 0.28, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart)
		for _, s in ipairs(UIK.shadows) do s.Visible = not minimized end
	end)

	-- круглая кнопка-логотип, когда окно скрыто
	bubble = make("TextButton", {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(46, 46), Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0.5, -23), BackgroundColor3 = C.bg, BackgroundTransparency = 0.15,
		AutoButtonColor = false, Text = "", Visible = false, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 20}, screenGui)
	round(bubble, 23)
	stroke(bubble, C.line, 1, 0.75)
	Icon("claude", bubble, 24, C.accent, {AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), ZIndex = 21})
	local bs = make("UIScale", {Scale = 1}, bubble)
	tween(bs, {Scale = 1.06}, 1.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, -1, true)
	bubble.MouseEnter:Connect(function() overBubble = true end)
	bubble.MouseLeave:Connect(function() overBubble = false end)

	hidden = false
	setHidden = function(h)
		hidden = h
		bubble.Visible = h
		if h then
			MainFrame.Visible = false
			overMain = false
		else
			overBubble = false
			MainFrame.Visible = true
			uiScale.Scale = userScale * 0.85
			tween(uiScale, {Scale = userScale}, 0.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
		end
	end
	closeB.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setHidden(true) end)
	API.setHidden = function(h) setHidden(h) end
	bubble.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setHidden(false) end)
	connect(UserInputService.InputBegan, function(input, processed)
		if processed or introActive then return end
		if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.RightShift or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.H then
			setHidden(not hidden)
		elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.End then
			API.panic()
		elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Slash and MainFrame.Visible and UIK.prompt then
			task.defer(function() UIK.prompt:CaptureFocus() end)
		elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Slash or (input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.K and (UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl) or UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.RightControl))) then
			UIK.openPalette()
		elseif (input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.LeftBracket or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.RightBracket) and MainFrame.Visible then
			local idx = 1
			for i, tb in ipairs(TABS) do
				if tb[1] == currentTab then idx = i end
			end
			idx = idx + ((input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.RightBracket) and 1 or -1)
			selectTab(TABS[(idx - 1) % #TABS + 1][1])
		elseif API.onKey(input.KeyCode) then
			-- обработано в API
		end
	end)

	selectTab = function(name, instant)
		local changed = (currentTab ~= name)
		for key, page in pairs(pages) do
			if key == name then
				page.Visible = true
				if not instant and changed then
					page.Position = UDim2.new(0, 30, 0, 0)
					tween(page, {Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)}, 0.28, Enum.EasingStyle.Cubic)
				end
			else
				page.Visible = false
			end
		end
		for key, t in pairs(tabBtns) do
			local on = (key == name)
			tween(t.btn, {BackgroundTransparency = on and 0.86 or 1}, 0.15)
			if t.lbl then tween(t.lbl, {TextColor3 = on and C.accent or C.dim}, 0.15) end
			t.icon.SetColor(on and C.accent or C.iconDim, 0.15)
			if on then tween(UIK.ind, {Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, t.y + 7)}, instant and 0.01 or 0.28, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart) end
		end
		currentTab = name
		API.cfg.lastTab = name
		API.save()
		if changed then
			if name == "ballistics" then
				if API.balSet then API.balSet(true) end
			elseif API.balOn and API.balSet and not (name == "fpv" and API.fpv and API.fpv.S.body == "pallets") then
				API.balSet(false) -- ушёл с вкладки — баллистика выключается, ракеты снова доступны питомцам и ручной ракете
			end
			if name == "rockets" and ui.activateManual then ui.activateManual()
			elseif name == "swarm" and ui.activatePet then ui.activatePet() end
		end
	end
end

-- ---------- конструкторы страниц и элементов (стиль Oblivion: две колонки, сворачиваемые секции) ----------
local function scrollList(parent, size, pos)
	local sf = make("ScrollingFrame", {Size = size, Position = pos or UDim2.new(), BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0,
		ScrollBarThickness = 2, ScrollBarImageColor3 = C.white, ScrollBarImageTransparency = 0.6, CanvasSize = UDim2.new(),
		AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y}, parent)
	make("UIListLayout", {Padding = UDim.new(0, 8), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder}, sf)
	make("UIPadding", {PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 2), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 8), PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 1), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 6)}, sf)
	return sf
end

-- секция-«выпадашка»: заголовок со стрелкой, по клику сворачивается
UIK.secN = 0
function UIK.section(parent, title, order, collapsed)
	UIK.secN += 1
	local f = make("Frame", {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, BackgroundColor3 = C.panel, BackgroundTransparency = 0.45,
		BorderSizePixel = 0, LayoutOrder = order or 0, ClipsDescendants = true}, parent)
	round(f, 8)
	stroke(f, C.line, 1, 0.91)
	make("UIGradient", {Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Color3.fromRGB(236, 234, 229)), Rotation = 90}, f)
	make("UIListLayout", {SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder}, f)
	local head = make("TextButton", {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 32), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, LayoutOrder = 0}, f)
	local tl = make("TextLabel", {Size = UDim2.new(1, -44, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(11, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = title, Font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono,
		TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = C.text, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd}, head)
	local chev = Icon("chev", head, 14, C.icon, {AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0.5, 0)})
	local body = make("Frame", {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, BackgroundTransparency = 1, LayoutOrder = 1}, f)
	make("UIListLayout", {Padding = UDim.new(0, 5), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder}, body)
	make("UIPadding", {PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 10), PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 10), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 10)}, body)
	make("Frame", {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(2, 14), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 9), BackgroundColor3 = C.accent, BorderSizePixel = 0}, head)
	local key = "sec:" .. title
	local open = not collapsed
	if API.cfg[key] ~= nil then open = API.cfg[key] end
	local function render(anim)
		body.Visible = open
		if anim then tween(chev.frame, {Rotation = open and 0 or -90}, 0.15) else chev.frame.Rotation = open and 0 or -90 end
	end
	head.MouseEnter:Connect(function() tween(tl, {TextColor3 = C.accent}, 0.12) end)
	head.MouseLeave:Connect(function() tween(tl, {TextColor3 = C.text}, 0.12) end)
	head.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		open = not open
		API.cfg[key] = open
		API.save()
		render(true)
	end)
	render(false)
	return body, f, function(t) tl.Text = t end
end

-- страница из двух колонок: слева список режимов в секции, справа секции с настройками
UIK.heads = {}
local function newSplitPage(name, listTitle)
	local page = make("Frame", {Name = name, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Visible = false}, pagesHolder)
	local leftOuter = make("Frame", {Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -5, 1, -2), BackgroundColor3 = C.panel, BackgroundTransparency = 0.45, BorderSizePixel = 0}, page)
	round(leftOuter, 8)
	stroke(leftOuter, C.line, 1, 0.91)
	local head = make("TextButton", {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 32), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false}, leftOuter)
	local tl = make("TextLabel", {Size = UDim2.new(1, -44, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(11, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = listTitle or "Режимы", Font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono,
		TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = C.text, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd}, head)
	local chev = Icon("chev", head, 14, C.icon, {AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0.5, 0)})
	local left = make("Frame", {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -32), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 32), BackgroundTransparency = 1}, leftOuter)
	local open = true
	head.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		open = not open
		left.Visible = open
		tween(chev.frame, {Rotation = open and 0 or -90}, 0.15)
		tween(leftOuter, {Size = open and UDim2.new(0.5, -5, 1, -2) or UDim2.new(0.5, -5, 0, 32)}, 0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart)
	end)
	UIK.heads[left] = function(t) tl.Text = t end
	local right = scrollList(page, UDim2.new(0.5, -5, 1, 0), UDim2.new(0.5, 5, 0, 0))
	pages[name] = page
	return left, right
end

-- обычная страница: две независимые колонки секций
function UIK.newTwoColPage(name)
	local page = make("Frame", {Name = name, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Visible = false}, pagesHolder)
	local colL = scrollList(page, UDim2.new(0.5, -5, 1, 0))
	local colR = scrollList(page, UDim2.new(0.5, -5, 1, 0), UDim2.new(0.5, 5, 0, 0))
	pages[name] = page
	return colL, colR, page
end

UIK.listN = 0
local function buildModeList(left, modes, onSelect)
	UIK.listN += 1
	local lid = UIK.listN
	local search = make("TextBox", {Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 26), Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0), BackgroundColor3 = C.el, BackgroundTransparency = 0.5,
		PlaceholderText = "Найти режим", PlaceholderColor3 = C.iconDim, Text = "", TextColor3 = C.text, ClearTextOnFocus = false,
		Font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono, TextSize = 12, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, BorderSizePixel = 0}, left)
	round(search, 13)
	stroke(search, C.line, 1, 0.92)
	make("UIPadding", {PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 28)}, search)
	Icon("search", search, 12, C.iconDim, {AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0, -19, 0.5, 0)})
	local list = make("ScrollingFrame", {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -34), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 32), BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0, ScrollBarThickness = 2, ScrollBarImageColor3 = C.white, ScrollBarImageTransparency = 0.6, CanvasSize = UDim2.new(), AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y}, left)
	make("UIListLayout", {Padding = UDim.new(0, 2), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder}, list)
	make("UIPadding", {PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 6), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 8), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 8)}, list)
	local buttons, rows, lowered, labels = {}, {}, {}, {}
	for i, m in ipairs(modes) do
		local fk = lid .. ":" .. m.key
		local row = make("Frame", {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26 + UIK.thv), BackgroundTransparency = 1, LayoutOrder = i}, list)
		local b = make("TextButton", {Size = UDim2.new(1, -26, 1, 0), BackgroundColor3 = C.line, BackgroundTransparency = 1, AutoButtonColor = false, Text = m.label,
			TextColor3 = C.dim, Font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono, TextSize = 12, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, BorderSizePixel = 0, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd}, row)
		round(b, 6)
		make("UIPadding", {PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 8)}, b)
		b.MouseEnter:Connect(function() if b:GetAttribute("Sel") ~= true then tween(b, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.95, TextColor3 = C.text}, 0.1) end end)
		b.MouseLeave:Connect(function() if b:GetAttribute("Sel") ~= true then tween(b, {BackgroundTransparency = 1, TextColor3 = C.dim}, 0.1) end end)
		UIK.tip(b, m.label .. "\n" .. (m.desc or ""))
		b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() onSelect(m.key) end)
		local star = make("TextButton", {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(22, 22), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.5, 0),
			BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false}, row)
		local starIc
		local function paintStar()
			local on = API.cfg.fav[fk] == true
			if starIc then starIc.frame:Destroy() end
			starIc = Icon(on and "starFill" or "star", star, 13, on and C.accent or C.iconDim, {AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)})
			row.LayoutOrder = on and (i - 10000) or i
		end
		star.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			API.cfg.fav[fk] = (API.cfg.fav[fk] ~= true) or nil
			API.save()
			paintStar()
		end)
		paintStar()
		buttons[m.key], rows[m.key], labels[m.key] = b, row, m.label
		lowered[m.key] = ulower(m.label) .. " " .. m.key:lower()
	end
	search:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
		local q = ulower(search.Text)
		for key, row in pairs(rows) do
			row.Visible = (q == "") or (lowered[key]:find(q, 1, true) ~= nil)
		end
	end)
	return function(selected)
		for key, b in pairs(buttons) do
			local on = (key == selected)
			b:SetAttribute("Sel", on)
			tween(b, {BackgroundTransparency = on and 0.9 or 1, TextColor3 = on and C.accent or C.dim}, 0.12)
			b.Font = on and Enum.Font.RobotoMono or Enum.Font.RobotoMono
		end
		if UIK.heads[left] and labels[selected] then UIK.heads[left](labels[selected]) end
	end
end

local function cardText(parent, text, size, color, font, order)
	if color == C.accent then color = C.white end
	if font == Enum.Font.GothamBlack then font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono end
	return make("TextLabel", {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Text = text, TextWrapped = true, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top,
		TextSize = math.min(size, 14), TextColor3 = color, Font = font, LayoutOrder = order or 0}, parent)
end

local function slider(parent, scroll, name, min, max, default, step, order, onChange)
	local holder = make("Frame", {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36 + UIK.thv), BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, LayoutOrder = order}, parent)
	local nameLbl = make("TextLabel", {Size = UDim2.new(1, -60, 0, 18), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = name, TextColor3 = C.dim,
		Font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono, TextSize = 12, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd}, holder)
	local valLbl = make("TextLabel", {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(56, 18), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "",
		TextColor3 = C.text, Font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono, TextSize = 12, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right}, holder)
	local track = make("Frame", {Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 3), Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 26 + UIK.thv / 2), BackgroundColor3 = C.off, BorderSizePixel = 0}, holder)
	round(track, 2)
	local fill = make("Frame", {BackgroundColor3 = C.accent, BackgroundTransparency = 0.15, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)}, track)
	round(fill, 2)
	local knob = make("Frame", {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(11, 11), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0), BackgroundColor3 = C.white, BorderSizePixel = 0}, track)
	round(knob, 6)
	local ks = make("UIScale", {Scale = 1}, knob)
	local hit = make("TextButton", {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, -15), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = ""}, track)
	local value = default
	local function render()
		local r = (value - min) / (max - min)
		fill.Size = UDim2.new(r, 0, 1, 0)
		knob.Position = UDim2.new(r, 0, 0.5, 0)
		valLbl.Text = tostring(value)
	end
	local function setValue(v, fire)
		v = math.clamp(math.floor(v / step + 0.5) * step, min, max)
		value = math.floor(v * 1000 + 0.5) / 1000
		render()
		if fire and onChange then onChange(value) end
	end
	local function fromX(x)
		setValue(min + (max - min) * math.clamp((x - track.AbsolutePosition.X) / track.AbsoluteSize.X, 0, 1), true)
	end
	local dragging = false
	hit.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			dragging = true
			scroll.ScrollingEnabled = false
			tween(ks, {Scale = 1.3}, 0.1)
			fromX(input.Position.X)
		end
	end)
	connect(UserInputService.InputChanged, function(input)
		if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then fromX(input.Position.X) end
	end)
	connect(UserInputService.InputEnded, function(input)
		if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
			dragging = false
			scroll.ScrollingEnabled = true
			tween(ks, {Scale = 1}, 0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
		end
	end)
	render()
	return {Get = function() return value end, Set = function(v) setValue(v, false) end, SetName = function(n) nameLbl.Text = n end}
end

local function toggle(parent, text, default, order, cb) -- строка с текстом и маленьким круглым переключателем справа
	local row = make("TextButton", {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24 + UIK.thv), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BorderSizePixel = 0, LayoutOrder = order}, parent)
	local lbl = make("TextLabel", {Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 1, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = text, TextWrapped = true, TextColor3 = C.dim,
		Font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono, TextSize = 12, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left}, row)
	local sw = make("Frame", {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(26, 14), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -2, 0.5, 0), BackgroundColor3 = C.off, BorderSizePixel = 0}, row)
	round(sw, 7)
	local swS = stroke(sw, C.line, 1, 0.85)
	local knob = make("Frame", {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 10), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, 0), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 156), BorderSizePixel = 0}, sw)
	round(knob, 5)
	local swScale = make("UIScale", {Scale = 1}, sw)
	local state = default
	-- наведение: подпись и рамка переключателя оживают; нажатие: переключатель «пружинит»
	row.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
		tween(lbl, {TextColor3 = C.text}, 0.1)
		if not state then tween(swS, {Color = C.accent, Transparency = 0.5}, 0.1) end
	end)
	row.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
		if not state then tween(lbl, {TextColor3 = C.dim}, 0.1) end
		tween(swS, {Color = C.line, Transparency = state and 1 or 0.85}, 0.1)
		tween(swScale, {Scale = 1}, 0.15)
	end)
	row.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function() tween(swScale, {Scale = 0.86}, 0.07) end)
	row.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function() tween(swScale, {Scale = 1}, 0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Back) end)
	local function render()
		tween(sw, {BackgroundColor3 = state and C.accent or C.off}, 0.15)
		swS.Transparency = state and 1 or 0.85
		tween(lbl, {TextColor3 = state and C.text or C.dim}, 0.15)
		tween(knob, {Position = state and UDim2.new(1, -12, 0.5, 0) or UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, 0), BackgroundColor3 = state and C.bg or Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 156)}, 0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart)
	end
	row.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() state = not state; render(); cb(state) end)
	render()
	return {Set = function(v) state = v; render() end}
end

UIK.load(0.5, "Вкладки и режимы…")
-- ---------- ВКЛАДКА: РАКЕТА (одиночная) ----------
do
	local left, right = newSplitPage("rockets", "Режим полёта")

	ui.rkStatus = cardText(sInfo, "Статус: ищу ракету (BombMissile) рядом…", 12, C.text, Enum.Font.RobotoMono, 3)
