	slider(sPhy, colR, "Тяга / вес", 2, 8, S.twr, 0.1, 1, function(v) S.twr = v; save() end)
	slider(sPhy, colR, "Ветер и порывы", 0, 1, S.wind, 0.05, 2, function(v) S.wind = v; save() end)
	slider(sPhy, colR, "Ёмкость батареи, x", 0.5, 3, S.batMul, 0.1, 3, function(v) S.batMul = v; save() end)
	toggle(sPhy, "Airmode (управление на нулевом газе)", S.airmode, 4, function(v) S.airmode = v; save() end)
	toggle(sPhy, "Реальные краши (дизарм при ударе)", S.crashes, 5, function(v) S.crashes = v; save() end)
	cardText(sPhy, "Гоночный дрон — тяга/вес 5–8, фристайл — 3–4, синематик — 2. Чем больше тяга, тем ниже газ висения.", 11, C.iconDim, Enum.Font.RobotoMono, 6)

	local sAs = UIK.section(colR, "Помощники Claude", 2)
	for i, t in ipairs({
		{"guard", "Claude Guard — уводит от стен (ACRO++)"}, {"predict", "Линия прогноза траектории (ACRO++)"},
		{"rescue", "GPS Rescue: при потере сигнала летит домой"}, {"slowmo", "Slow-mo на правую кнопку мыши"},
		{"tricks", "Трюки, комбо и очки"}, {"trail", "Шлейф за дроном"}, {"sound", "Звук ветра и ударов"},
	}) do
		toggle(sAs, t[2], S[t[1]], i, function(v) S[t[1]] = v; save() end)
	end

	local sRing = UIK.section(colR, "Свои кольца", 3)
	cardText(sRing, "Ставь кольца сам: G в полёте — впереди дрона, G на земле (режим FPV) — перед тобой по взгляду камеры. Пролетай по номерам — это трасса для тебя и для ИИ-пилота. Видишь их только ты.", 11, C.dim, Enum.Font.RobotoMono, 0)
	slider(sRing, colR, "Размер кольца", 10, 40, S.ringSize, 1, 1, function(v) S.ringSize = v; save() end)
	local grg = grid2(sRing, 2)
	button(grg, "Поставить кольцо  G", C.accentDark, 1).MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() F.placeRing() end)
	button(grg, "Показать мои кольца", C.el, 2).MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		if #F.rings == 0 then notify("Своих колец пока нет", C.danger) return end
		F.rebuildRings()
		notify(("Мои кольца: %d"):format(#F.rings))
	end)
	button(grg, "Убрать последнее", C.el, 3).MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() F.undoRing() end)
	button(grg, "Убрать все", C.el, 4).MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() F.clearRings() end)
	toggle(sRing, "Запоминать кольца для этой игры", S.keepRings, 3, function(v) S.keepRings = v; F.saveRings() end)

	local sRace = UIK.section(colR, "Случайная трасса", 4)
	cardText(sRace, "Квадратные ворота по кругу вокруг тебя. Ворота 1 — старт и финиш. Лучший круг летит рядом призраком.", 11, C.dim, Enum.Font.RobotoMono, 0)
	slider(sRace, colR, "Ворот на трассе", 4, 16, S.gates, 1, 1, function(v) S.gates = v; save() end)
	local gr = grid2(sRace, 2)
	button(gr, "Построить трассу", C.accentDark, 1).MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() F.buildCourse(S.gates) end)
	button(gr, "Убрать трассу", C.el, 2).MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		F.clearCourse()
		notify("Трасса убрана")
	end)
	local raceLbl = cardText(sRace, "", 12, C.text, Enum.Font.RobotoMono, 3)

	local sSt = UIK.section(colR, "Статистика пилота", 5)
	local stLbl = cardText(sSt, "", 12, C.text, Enum.Font.RobotoMono, 1)
	button(sSt, "Сбросить статистику", C.el, 2).MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		for key in pairs(ST) do ST[key] = 0 end
		API.save()
		notify("Статистика пилота сброшена")
	end)

	local sKeys = UIK.section(colR, "Клавиши в полёте", 6, true)
	cardText(sKeys, "F — взлететь / сесть   R — режим   T — черепаха (перевернуть после краша)\nP — ИИ-пилот   N — другая цель для ИИ   G — поставить кольцо\nB — GPS Rescue домой   ПКМ — slow-mo   колесо — наклон камеры\n\nACRO: мышь — тангаж/крен, A/D — рыскание, W/S — газ, Пробел — полный, Shift — ноль\nACRO++: мышь — нос и поворот, W/S — вверх/вниз, A/D — крен, Q/E — бочка, Z/C — флип, V — пируэт, Пробел — форсаж, Shift — тормоз", 11, C.dim, Enum.Font.RobotoMono, 1)

	local acc = 0
	connect(RunService.Heartbeat, function(dt)
		acc += dt
		if acc < 0.4 or not page.Visible then return end
		acc = 0
		local ready = swarmModeKey == "FPV"
		local m = bal20()
		if S.body == "pallets" then
			status.Text = m and ("Поддонов выбрано: %d/20  ·  %s  ·  %s"):format(m.pallets(), F.active and "в полёте" or (m.built() and "собран, ждёт взлёта" or "не собран"), F.NAMES[F.mode])
				or "Модуль баллистики не загрузился"
		else
			status.Text = ready and ("Ракет в дроне: %d/5  ·  %s  ·  %s"):format(F.count or 0, F.active and "в полёте" or "ждёт взлёта", F.NAMES[F.mode])
				or "Режим питомца сейчас не FPV — нажми «Собрать дрон»"
		end
		local c = F.course
		raceLbl.Text = (c and c.folder.Parent) and (((c.custom and "Мои кольца: %d" or "Ворот: %d") .. "  ·  кругов: %d  ·  лучший: %s"):format(c.n, c.lap, c.best and ("%.2f с"):format(c.best) or "—"))
			or "Трассы нет"
		local tt = math.floor(ST.time)
		stLbl.Text = ("Полётов: %d  ·  налёт %d:%02d\nМакс. скорость: %d км/ч  ·  пролетел %.2f км\nЛучший круг: %s  ·  рекорд очков: %d\nТрюков: %d  ·  крашей: %d"):format(
			ST.flights, math.floor(tt / 60), tt % 60, ST.maxSpd, ST.dist / 1000, (ST.bestLap > 0) and ("%.2f с"):format(ST.bestLap) or "—", ST.bestScore, ST.tricks, ST.crashes)
	end)
end)()

-- ---------- ВКЛАДКА: НАСТРОЙКИ ----------
local fakeBoxPart, fullBodyBox
local playIntro
do
	local colL, colR = UIK.newTwoColPage("settings")
	local sUi = UIK.section(colL, "Интерфейс", 1)
	UIK.grab.strict = API.cfg.strictGrab ~= false
	local sCfg = UIK.section(colL, "Конфиг Claude", 2)
	cardText(sCfg, "Включается сам при запуске: FOV 105, атмосфера «Закат», без рамки вокруг тела.", 11, C.dim, Enum.Font.RobotoMono, 0)
	button(sCfg, "Применить конфиг Claude ещё раз", C.accentDark, 1).MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		if API.setFov then API.setFov(105) end
		pcall(function() API.atmoSunset() end)
		showBodyBox = false
		if ui.boxT then ui.boxT.Set(false) end
		notify("Конфиг Claude: FOV 105 · закат · без рамки")
	end)
	local sGrab = UIK.section(colL, "Захват объектов", 2)
	cardText(sGrab, "Жёсткий захват: скрипт берёт ракету, спаркер или банан только после того, как ты сам взял его рукой и отпустил.", 11, C.dim, Enum.Font.RobotoMono, 0)
	toggle(sGrab, "Жёсткий захват (взял и отпустил)", UIK.grab.strict, 1, function(v)
		UIK.grab.strict = v
		API.cfg.strictGrab = v
		API.save()
	end)
	button(sGrab, "Разрешить всё, что рядом, прямо сейчас", C.el, 2).MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		notify("Разрешено объектов рядом: " .. UIK.grab.armNear())
	end)
	local sRfx = UIK.section(colL, "Ракеты: физика и эффекты", 2)
	toggle(sRfx, "Инерция: разгон, дуги, занос", true, 1, function(v) UIK.rfx.inertia = v end)
	toggle(sRfx, "Пламя, свет и шлейф из сопла", true, 2, function(v) UIK.rfx.on = v end)
	toggle(sRfx, "Дым за ракетами", true, 3, function(v) UIK.rfx.smoke = v end)
	toggle(sRfx, "Звуковой барьер: кольца на скорости", true, 4, function(v) UIK.rfx.booms = v end)
	toggle(sRfx, "Камера: обзор шире на скорости", true, 5, function(v)
		UIK.rfx.cam = v
		if not v and UIK.rfx.baseFov then camera.FieldOfView = UIK.rfx.baseFov; UIK.rfx.baseFov = nil end
	end)

	slider(sUi, colL, "Масштаб интерфейса", 0.6, 1.4, API.cfg.userMul or 1, 0.05, 1, function(v) userMul = v; userScale = autoScale() * v; uiScale.Scale = userScale; API.cfg.userMul = v; API.save() end)
	slider(sUi, colL, "Дальность захвата", 10, 150, 30, 1, 2, function(v) captureRange = v end)
	slider(sUi, colL, "Прозрачность окна", 0, 0.6, API.cfg.winT or 0.12, 0.02, 2, function(v) UIK.setWinT(v) end)
	slider(sUi, colL, "Прозрачность фона-картинки", 0.5, 1, API.cfg.bgT or 0.88, 0.02, 2, function(v) UIK.setBgT(v) end)
	toggle(sUi, "Прицел на экране", true, 3, function(v) showCrosshair = v; ui.cross.Visible = v end)
	ui.boxT = toggle(sUi, "Рамка вокруг тела", false, 4, function(v) showBodyBox = v end)
	toggle(sUi, "Экранные кнопки (для сенсора)", touchUiOn, 5, function(v) touchUiOn = v; API.cfg.touchUi = v; API.save() end)
	toggle(sUi, "Компактная панель (без нижней подписи)", UIK.compact == true, 5, function(v) UIK.setCompact(v) end)
	do
		local row = make("Frame", {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30 + UIK.thv), BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, LayoutOrder = 5}, sUi)
		make("TextLabel", {Size = UDim2.new(0.3, 0, 1, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "Интро", TextColor3 = C.dim,
			Font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono, TextSize = 12, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left}, row)
		local btns = {}
		local function paintIntro()
			for k, b in pairs(btns) do setBase(b, ((API.cfg.introMode or "full") == k) and C.accent or C.el) end
		end
		for i, o in ipairs({{"full", "Полное"}, {"short", "Быстрое"}, {"off", "Выкл"}}) do
			local b = make("TextButton", {Size = UDim2.new(0.22, 0, 0, 24), Position = UDim2.new(0.3 + (i - 1) * 0.235, 0, 0.5, -12), Text = o[2], Font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono,
				TextSize = 11, BorderSizePixel = 0, AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundColor3 = C.el, TextColor3 = C.text}, row)
			round(b, 12)
			b:SetAttribute("Base", C.el)
			attachHover(b)
			btns[o[1]] = b
			b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() API.cfg.introMode = o[1]; API.save(); paintIntro() end)
		end
		paintIntro()
	end

	local info = UIK.section(colR, "Подсказки", 6)
	cardText(info, "• RightShift: скрыть/показать окно\n• Ракета: подойди, потом кликни по ней\n• Вкладка «Моя ракета» включает питомца, F — действие режима\n• Камера: ЛКМ/ПКМ или палец вне окна\n• Спаркеры берутся так же, как ракета\n• Дальность захвата общая для всего", 12, C.dim, Enum.Font.RobotoMono, 2)

	local sScr = UIK.section(colR, "Скрипт", 7)
	local introBtn = button(sScr, "Показать интро", C.el, 7)
	introBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() if not introActive then playIntro(function() setHidden(false) end) end end)

	local unloadBtn = button(sScr, "Выгрузить скрипт", C.danger, 8)
	function API.unload()
		_G.ClaudeClientStop = nil
		pcall(function() API.fpv.clearCourse() end)
		pcall(function() UIK.loader.gui:Destroy() end)
		pcall(function() game:GetService("Lighting").ClaudeIntroBlur:Destroy() end)
		-- каждый шаг под защитой: если один упадёт, выгрузка всё равно дойдёт до конца
		pcall(function() if activeRocket then cancelRocket() end end)
		pcall(releaseAllSparklers)
		pcall(API.releaseAllSwarm)
		pcall(API.camRelease)
		pcall(API.laserClear)
		pcall(API.worldClear)
		pcall(API.playersClear)
		pcall(function() API.balModule.cleanup() end)
		pcall(function() API.fpv.exit(true) end)
		pcall(function() API.fpv.clearCourse() end)
		pcall(function() if ui.bark then ui.bark:Stop() end end)
		pcall(function() ui.bark:Destroy() end)
		pcall(function() if _G.HexAddonStop then _G.HexAddonStop() end end)
		pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("FembiixMain") end)
		for _, c in ipairs(conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
		pcall(function() fakeBoxPart:Destroy() end)
		pcall(function() fullBodyBox:Destroy() end)
		pcall(function() bhCore:Destroy() end)
		pcall(function() bhGlow:Destroy() end)
		pcall(function() API.aimCore:Destroy() end)
		for _, e in ipairs(wire.pool) do pcall(function() e.part:Destroy() end) end
		pcall(function() -- камера и мышь — как были в игре
			camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
			local h = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if h then camera.CameraSubject = h end
			UserInputService.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.Default
			UserInputService.MouseIconEnabled = true
		end)
		pcall(function() screenGui:Destroy() end)
	end
	_G.ClaudeClientStop = API.unload
	unloadBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(API.unload)
end

-- рамка вокруг тела
fakeBoxPart = make("Part", {Name = "FembiixBoxPart", Size = Vector3.new(3, 5, 3), Transparency = 1, CanCollide = false, Anchored = true, CanQuery = false, CanTouch = false}, workspace)
fullBodyBox = make("SelectionBox", {Name = "FembiixBox", Color3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 40, 20), LineThickness = 0.08, Adornee = fakeBoxPart})
pcall(function() fullBodyBox.Parent = CoreGui end)
if not fullBodyBox.Parent then fullBodyBox.Parent = screenGui end

-- ======================= ПОИСК-ПАЛИТРА (Ctrl+K), ПАНИКА, ПОМОЩЬ =======================
do
	function API.panic() -- всё остановить и вернуть как было
		pcall(function() API.balSet(false) end)
		pcall(function() API.fpv.exit(true) end)
		pcall(function() API.fpv.clearCourse() end)
		pcall(API.dismount)
		pcall(API.bobikCancel)
		pcall(API.setSwarmLaunched, false)
		pcall(API.releaseAllSwarm)
		pcall(releaseAllSparklers)
		pcall(API.bananaStop)
		pcall(API.camRelease)
		if activeRocket then pcall(cancelRocket) end
		notify("ПАНИКА: всё остановлено", C.danger)
	end

	local pal = make("Frame", {Name = "Palette", Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 0.5,
		Visible = false, ZIndex = 150, BorderSizePixel = 0}, screenGui)
	local back = make("TextButton", {Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false}, pal)
	local box = make("Frame", {AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 70), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(500, 380), BackgroundColor3 = C.bg, BackgroundTransparency = 0.05, BorderSizePixel = 0, Active = true}, pal)
	round(box, 12)
	stroke(box, C.line, 1, 0.85)
	make("UIScale", {Scale = math.clamp(camera.ViewportSize.X / 580, 0.6, 1)}, box)
	local tb = make("TextBox", {Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 38), Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 12), BackgroundColor3 = C.el, BackgroundTransparency = 0.4, Text = "", TextColor3 = C.text,
		PlaceholderText = "Режим, небо, шляпа, действие…   Enter — выбрать · Esc — закрыть", PlaceholderColor3 = C.dim, Font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono, TextSize = 13,
		ClearTextOnFocus = false, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, BorderSizePixel = 0}, box)
	round(tb, 19)
	stroke(tb, C.line, 1, 0.9)
	make("UIPadding", {PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 36)}, tb)
	Icon("search", tb, 15, C.iconDim, {AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0, -24, 0.5, 0)})
	local res = make("ScrollingFrame", {Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, -70), Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 62), BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0,
		ScrollBarThickness = 2, ScrollBarImageColor3 = C.white, ScrollBarImageTransparency = 0.6, CanvasSize = UDim2.new(), AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y}, box)
	make("UIListLayout", {Padding = UDim.new(0, 3), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder}, res)

	local items, shown, rows, sel = {}, {}, {}, 1
	local function buildItems()
		local L = {}
		local function add(label, kw, fn, sub) L[#L + 1] = {label = label, kw = ulower(label .. " " .. kw), fn = fn, sub = sub} end
		for _, t in ipairs(TABS) do add("Вкладка: " .. t[3], "вкладка tab открыть", function() selectTab(t[1]) end) end
		for _, m in ipairs(ROCKET_MODES) do add("Ракета: " .. m.label, "ручная ракета полёт " .. m.key, function() selectTab("rockets"); ui.selectRocketMode(m.key) end, m.desc) end
		for _, m in ipairs(SWARM_MODES) do add("Питомец: " .. m.label, "моя ракета питомец " .. m.key, function() selectTab("swarm"); ui.selectSwarmMode(m.key) end, m.desc) end
		for _, m in ipairs(SPARK_MODES) do add("Огни: " .. m.label, "спаркеры огни " .. m.key, function() selectTab("sparklers"); ui.selectSparkMode(m.key) end, m.desc) end
		if API.worldItems then
			for _, it in ipairs(API.worldItems()) do add("Мир: " .. it[1], "мир небо шляпа камера", function() selectTab("world"); it[2]() end) end
		end
		add("Паника: всё выключить", "стоп остановить панику end", API.panic)
		add("Компактная панель вкл/выкл", "компакт иконки панель", function() UIK.setCompact(not UIK.compact) end)
		add("Горячие клавиши", "помощь клавиши help", function() UIK.showHelp() end)
		add("Центрировать окно", "окно центр позиция", function() MainFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0) end)
		add("Скрыть окно", "скрыть спрятать rightshift", function() setHidden(true) end)
		add("Показать интро", "интро заставка", function() playIntro(function() setHidden(false); UIK.entrance() end) end)
		return L
	end
	local function paint()
		for i, r in ipairs(rows) do
			r.BackgroundTransparency = (i == sel) and 0.92 or 1
			r.BackgroundColor3 = C.white
		end
		if rows[sel] then res.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, math.max(0, (sel - 4) * 34)) end
	end
	local function close() pal.Visible = false; tb:ReleaseFocus() end
	local function run(i)
		local it = shown[i]
		if not it then return end
		close()
		local ok, err = pcall(it.fn)
		if not ok then warn("[Claude] палитра: " .. tostring(err)) end
	end
	local function refresh()
		for _, r in ipairs(rows) do r:Destroy() end
		rows, shown = {}, {}
		local toks = {}
		for w in ulower(tb.Text):gmatch("%S+") do toks[#toks + 1] = w end
		for _, it in ipairs(items) do
			local ok = true
			for _, w in ipairs(toks) do
				if not it.kw:find(w, 1, true) then ok = false break end
			end
			if ok then shown[#shown + 1] = it end
			if #shown >= 40 then break end
		end
		sel = 1
		for i, it in ipairs(shown) do
			local b = make("TextButton", {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, it.sub and 42 or 32), BackgroundColor3 = C.accent, BackgroundTransparency = 1, AutoButtonColor = false, Text = "",
				BorderSizePixel = 0, LayoutOrder = i}, res)
			round(b, 9)
			make("TextLabel", {Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 22), Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, it.sub and 3 or 5), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = it.label, Font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono,
				TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = C.text, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd}, b)
			if it.sub then
				make("TextLabel", {Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 14), Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 24), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = it.sub, Font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono, TextSize = 10,
					TextColor3 = C.dim, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd}, b)
			end
			b.MouseEnter:Connect(function() sel = i; paint() end)
			b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() run(i) end)
			rows[i] = b
		end
		paint()
	end
	function UIK.runQuery(q)
		local toks = {}
		for w in ulower(q):gmatch("%S+") do toks[#toks + 1] = w end
		if #toks == 0 then return nil end
		for _, it in ipairs(buildItems()) do
			local ok = true
			for _, w in ipairs(toks) do
				if not it.kw:find(w, 1, true) then ok = false break end
			end
			if ok then
				pcall(it.fn)
				return it.label
			end
		end
		return nil
	end
	function UIK.runPrompt(q)
		local c = ulower(q):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
		local cmds = {
			["/help"] = function() UIK.showHelp() end, ["?"] = function() UIK.showHelp() end,
			["/panic"] = function() API.panic() end, ["/hide"] = function() setHidden(true) end,
			["/center"] = function() MainFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0); API.cfg.winPos = nil; API.save() end,
			["/intro"] = function() playIntro(function() setHidden(false); UIK.entrance() end) end,
			["/grab"] = function()
				UIK.grab.strict = not UIK.grab.strict
				API.cfg.strictGrab = UIK.grab.strict
				API.save()
				notify("Жёсткий захват: " .. (UIK.grab.strict and "вкл" or "выкл"))
			end,
			["/allow"] = function() notify("Разрешено объектов рядом: " .. UIK.grab.armNear()) end,
			["/bal"] = function() API.balSet(not API.balOn) end,
		}
		if cmds[c] then cmds[c]() return end
		if c:sub(1, 1) == "/" then notify("Нет такой команды: " .. c .. "  (/help)", C.danger) return end
		local label = UIK.runQuery(q)
		if label then notify("Готово: " .. label) else notify("Не нашёл: " .. q, C.danger) end
	end
	function UIK.openPalette()
		items = buildItems()
		pal.Visible = true
		tb.Text = ""
		refresh()
		box.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 40)
		tween(box, {Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 70)}, 0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
		task.defer(function() tb:CaptureFocus(); tb.Text = "" end)
	end
	tb:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(refresh)
	back.MouseButton1Click:Connect(close)
	connect(UserInputService.InputBegan, function(input)
		if not pal.Visible then return end
		if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Down then sel = math.min(sel + 1, #shown); paint()
		elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Up then sel = math.max(sel - 1, 1); paint()
		elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Return then run(sel)
		elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape then close() end
	end)

	-- окно «Горячие клавиши»
	local help = make("TextButton", {Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 0.5, Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false,
		Visible = false, ZIndex = 160, BorderSizePixel = 0}, screenGui)
	local hb2 = make("Frame", {AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(470, 400), BackgroundColor3 = C.bg, BackgroundTransparency = 0.05, BorderSizePixel = 0}, help)
	round(hb2, 12)
	stroke(hb2, C.line, 1, 0.85)
	make("UIScale", {Scale = math.clamp(camera.ViewportSize.X / 520, 0.6, 1)}, hb2)
	Icon("keyboard", hb2, 18, C.white, {Position = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 18)})
	make("TextLabel", {Size = UDim2.new(1, -60, 0, 30), Position = UDim2.new(0, 42, 0, 12), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "Горячие клавиши", Font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono, TextSize = 16,
		TextColor3 = C.white, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left}, hb2)
	make("TextLabel", {Size = UDim2.new(1, -32, 1, -60), Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 48), BackgroundTransparency = 1, TextWrapped = true, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
		TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top, Font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono, TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = C.text,
		Text = "Строка «>» внизу окна — пиши режим («дракон», «закат») или команду, Enter.\nКоманды: /help /panic /hide /center /intro /grab /allow\n\nRightShift / H — скрыть и показать окно\n/ — в строку «>»  ·  Ctrl+K — поиск по всему\n[  и  ] — предыдущая / следующая вкладка\nEnd — ПАНИКА: всё выключить\nF — действие питомца (залп, сесть, Бобик, огонь дракона)\nF — стоп бананов\nG — ехать к точке прицела при катании\nX — отмена Бобика\nПКМ/ЛКМ по земле — ехать туда при катании\n\nЗвёздочка у режима — в избранное, оно поднимается наверх.\nНажми, чтобы закрыть."}, hb2)
	function UIK.showHelp() help.Visible = true end
	help.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() help.Visible = false end)
end

ui.selectRocketMode("MANUAL")
ui.selectSwarmMode("FOLLOW")
ui.selectSparkMode("HEART")
selectTab((pages[API.cfg.lastTab or ""] and API.cfg.lastTab ~= "swarm" and API.cfg.lastTab ~= "ballistics") and API.cfg.lastTab or "rockets", true)

UIK.load(0.8, "Интро и поиск…")
-- ============================ ИНТРО 3.0 ============================
function UIK.entrance() -- вкладки по очереди въезжают слева
	for i, tb in ipairs(TABS) do
		local t = tabBtns[tb[1]]
		t.btn.Position = UDim2.new(0, -220, 0, t.y)
		task.delay(0.06 * i, function() tween(t.btn, {Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, t.y)}, 0.45, Enum.EasingStyle.Back) end)
	end
end

-- Интро в стиле Claude Code: логотип вырастает луч за лучом, имя печатается с курсором,
-- появляется приветственная рамка, модули грузятся строками как в терминале, затем всё плавно уходит.
playIntro = function(onDone)
	introActive = true
	MainFrame.Visible = false
	bubble.Visible = false
	local K = (API.cfg.introMode == "short") and 0.5 or 1
	local skipped, finished = false, false
	local iconns = {}
	local vp = camera.ViewportSize
	local t0 = os.clock()
	local S = {} -- всё, что двигается покадрово

	local ov = make("CanvasGroup", {Name = "Intro", Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0,
		ZIndex = 100, Active = true, GroupTransparency = 1}, screenGui)
	tween(ov, {GroupTransparency = 0}, 0.45) -- мягко проявляется из загрузчика
	S.blur = make("BlurEffect", {Name = "ClaudeIntroBlur", Size = 0}, game:GetService("Lighting")) -- мир не размываем (компактное окно)
	-- компактное тёмное окно по центру — вместо белого фона на весь экран
	local cs = math.clamp(math.min(vp.Y / 1000, vp.X / 1100), 0.4, 0.75)
	local card = make("Frame", {AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(600 * cs, 600 * cs),
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(24, 22, 20), BackgroundTransparency = 0.04, BorderSizePixel = 0, ClipsDescendants = true}, ov)
	round(card, 18)
	stroke(card, C.accent, 1.5, 0.35)
	make("UIGradient", {Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), Color3.fromRGB(200, 190, 180)), Rotation = 90}, card)
	-- тонкая сетка «миллиметровки», медленно ползёт
	local gx, gy = math.ceil(vp.X / 56) + 2, math.ceil(vp.Y / 56) + 2
	S.grid = make("Frame", {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(gx * 56, gy * 56), BackgroundTransparency = 1}, card)
	for i = 0, gy do
		make("Frame", {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, i * 56), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 0.96, BorderSizePixel = 0}, S.grid)
	end
	for i = 0, gx do
		make("Frame", {Size = UDim2.new(0, 1, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(i * 56, 0), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 0.96, BorderSizePixel = 0}, S.grid)
	end
	-- тёплые пятна-боке плывут вверх
	S.bokeh = {}
	for i = 1, 22 do
		local sz = math.random(14, 110)
		local f = make("Frame", {AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(sz, sz), BorderSizePixel = 0, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			BackgroundColor3 = (i % 3 == 0) and C.accent or Color3.fromRGB(242, 196, 172)}, card)
		round(f, 200)
		S.bokeh[i] = {f = f, x = math.random(), y = math.random(), sp = 0.015 + math.random() * 0.035, ph = math.random() * 6.28, tr = 0.78 + math.random() * 0.17}
	end

	local stack = make("Frame", {AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, -37 * cs), Size = UDim2.new(), BackgroundTransparency = 1, ZIndex = 5}, card)
	S.uis = make("UIScale", {Scale = cs}, stack)

	-- логотип Claude: 12 лучей разной длины вырастают по кругу
	local LS = 150
	local logo = make("Frame", {AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, -170), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(LS, LS), BackgroundTransparency = 1}, stack)
	S.glow = make("Frame", {AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), BackgroundColor3 = C.accent,
		BackgroundTransparency = 0.88, BorderSizePixel = 0}, logo)
	round(S.glow, 400)
	S.glow2 = make("Frame", {AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), BackgroundColor3 = C.accent,
		BackgroundTransparency = 0.95, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 0}, logo) -- второй, широкий ореол свечения
	round(S.glow2, 600)
	local LEN = {10.2, 7.6, 10.8, 8.4, 9.9, 7.3, 10.9, 8.6, 9.6, 7.8, 10.5, 8.2}
	S.rays = {}
	for i = 1, 12 do
		local r = make("Frame", {AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 13), BackgroundColor3 = C.accent,
			BorderSizePixel = 0, BackgroundTransparency = 1}, logo)
		round(r, 7)
		S.rays[i] = {f = r, a = math.rad(i * 30 - 90 + ((i % 2 == 0) and 3 or -2)), len = LEN[i] / 24 * LS, start = 0.35 + (i - 1) * 0.055}
	end
	S.spin, S.scale = -100, 1

	-- имя: печатается моноширинным шрифтом с мигающим курсором
	local nameRow = make("Frame", {AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, -42), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 60), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X,
		BackgroundTransparency = 1}, stack)
	make("UIListLayout", {FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal, VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center, Padding = UDim.new(0, 6), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder}, nameRow)
	local nameL = make("TextLabel", {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 60), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", Font = Enum.Font.Merriweather,
		TextSize = 52, TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(245, 240, 232), LayoutOrder = 1}, nameRow)
	S.cursor = make("Frame", {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 46), BackgroundColor3 = C.accent, BorderSizePixel = 0, LayoutOrder = 2}, nameRow)
	local sub = make("TextLabel", {AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 2), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(560, 18), BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Text = "c l i e n t   ·   f t a p   ·   v 1 . 0", Font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono, TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = C.dim, TextTransparency = 1}, stack)

	-- рамка приветствия, как в Claude Code
	local box = make("CanvasGroup", {AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 46), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(500, 92), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 37, 34),
		BackgroundTransparency = 0.25, BorderSizePixel = 0, GroupTransparency = 1}, stack)
	round(box, 10)
	stroke(box, C.accent, 1.5, 0.15)
	Icon("claude", box, 18, C.accent, {Position = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 15)})
	make("TextLabel", {Size = UDim2.new(1, -60, 0, 22), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(44, 12), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "Welcome to Claude Client!", Font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono,
		TextSize = 15, TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(245, 240, 232), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left}, box)
	make("TextLabel", {Size = UDim2.new(1, -60, 0, 18), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(44, 40), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "/help — помощь  ·  Ctrl+K — поиск  ·  / — строка команд",
		Font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono, TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = C.dim, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left}, box)
	make("TextLabel", {Size = UDim2.new(1, -60, 0, 18), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(44, 60), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "cwd: ftap/" .. player.Name,
		Font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono, TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = C.iconDim, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left}, box)

	-- строки загрузки модулей: точка → крутилка → готово
	local ROWS = {
		{"Ручная ракета", #ROCKET_MODES .. " режимов полёта"},
		{"Питомцы и FPV-дрон", #SWARM_MODES .. " режимов"},
		{"Спаркеры", #SPARK_MODES .. " фигур огня"},
		{"Мир и атмосфера", "небо, молнии, шляпы"},
		{"Баллистика", "пусковая, турель, ПВО, дроны"},
	}
	S.rows = {}
	for i, r in ipairs(ROWS) do
		local f = make("CanvasGroup", {AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 156 + (i - 1) * 26), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(500, 22),
			BackgroundTransparency = 1, GroupTransparency = 1}, stack)
		local dot = make("Frame", {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 8), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0.5, 0), BackgroundColor3 = C.accent, BorderSizePixel = 0}, f)
		round(dot, 4)
		local spin = Icon("claude", f, 14, C.accent, {AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0.5, 0), Visible = false})
		local ok = Icon("check", f, 14, C.accent, {AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0.5, 0), Visible = false})
		make("TextLabel", {Size = UDim2.new(0.55, -24, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(26, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = r[1], Font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono,
			TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(245, 240, 232), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left}, f)
		local st = make("TextLabel", {Size = UDim2.new(0.45, 0, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.55, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", Font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono,
			TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = C.dim, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right}, f)
		S.rows[i] = {f = f, dot = dot, spin = spin, ok = ok, st = st, info = r[2]}
	end
	-- «думающая» строка с бегущим бликом, как статус Claude Code
	local think = make("TextLabel", {AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 300), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(500, 20), BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Text = "Запускаю Claude Client…", Font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono, TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), TextTransparency = 1}, stack)
	S.shine = make("UIGradient", {Color = ColorSequence.new({ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, C.dim), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.42, C.dim),
		ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, C.accent), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.58, C.dim), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, C.dim)}), Offset = Vector2.new(-1, 0)}, think)
	local skipB = make("TextButton", {AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1), Position = UDim2.new(1, -18, 1, -16), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(170, 30), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 37, 34),
		BackgroundTransparency = 0.3, BorderSizePixel = 0, Text = (DEVICE == "ПК") and "esc — пропустить" or "пропустить", Font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono, TextSize = 12,
		TextColor3 = C.dim, AutoButtonColor = false, ZIndex = 7}, ov)
	round(skipB, 8)
	stroke(skipB, C.line, 1, 0.85)

	-- покадровая анимация
	local function ease(x) x = math.clamp(x, 0, 1) return 1 - (1 - x) ^ 3 end
	local function back(x)
		x = math.clamp(x, 0, 1)
		local c = 1.9
		return 1 + (c + 1) * (x - 1) ^ 3 + c * (x - 1) ^ 2
	end
	table.insert(iconns, RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
		local t = (os.clock() - t0) / K
		S.grid.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-((t * 9) % 56), -((t * 5) % 56))
		for _, b in ipairs(S.bokeh) do
			b.y = (b.y - b.sp * dt) % 1.2
			b.f.Position = UDim2.fromScale(b.x + math.sin(t * 0.6 + b.ph) * 0.02, b.y - 0.1)
			b.f.BackgroundTransparency = 1 - (1 - b.tr) * ease(t / 1.2)
		end
		-- луч за лучом, а весь знак докручивается и «дышит»
		S.spin = -100 * (1 - back(t / 1.25))
		local breathe = 1 + 0.035 * math.sin(t * 2.6) * ease((t - 1.3) / 0.5)
		for _, r in ipairs(S.rays) do
			local p = back((t - r.start) / 0.32)
			local len = math.max(r.len * p, 0)
			local a = r.a + math.rad(S.spin)
			local c = (LS / 24 * 1.2 + len / 2) * breathe
			r.f.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(len * breathe, 13)
			r.f.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, math.cos(a) * c, 0.5, math.sin(a) * c)
			r.f.Rotation = math.deg(a)
			r.f.BackgroundTransparency = (p > 0.01) and 0 or 1
		end
		local g = ease((t - 0.9) / 0.6)
		S.glow.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(LS * 1.6 * g * (1 + 0.05 * math.sin(t * 2)), LS * 1.6 * g * (1 + 0.05 * math.sin(t * 2)))
		local g2 = LS * 2.6 * g * (1 + 0.1 * math.sin(t * 1.4))
		S.glow2.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(g2, g2)
		S.glow2.BackgroundTransparency = 0.93 + 0.03 * math.sin(t * 1.4)
		S.cursor.BackgroundTransparency = (math.floor(t * 2.2) % 2 == 0) and 0 or 1
		S.shine.Offset = Vector2.new(((t * 0.7) % 2) - 1, 0)
		for _, r in ipairs(S.rows) do
			if r.spin.frame.Visible then r.spin.frame.Rotation = (t * 240) % 360 end
		end
	end))

	local function ring(size, th)
		local r = make("Frame", {AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, -170), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(60, 60), BackgroundTransparency = 1}, stack)
		round(r, 2000)
		local st = stroke(r, C.accent, th or 4, 0.1)
		tween(r, {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size)}, 0.9, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart)
		tween(st, {Transparency = 1, Thickness = 1}, 0.9)
		task.delay(0.95, function() r:Destroy() end)
	end
	local function sparks() -- искры разлетаются от знака
		for i = 1, 28 do
			local s = make("Frame", {AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, -170), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 6), BackgroundColor3 = C.accent,
				BorderSizePixel = 0}, stack)
			round(s, 3)
			local a, d = i / 28 * TAU + math.random() * 0.2, math.random(120, 260)
			tween(s, {Position = UDim2.fromOffset(math.cos(a) * d, -170 + math.sin(a) * d), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(2, 2), BackgroundTransparency = 1}, 0.9 + math.random() * 0.4, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart)
			task.delay(1.4, function() s:Destroy() end)
		end
	end
	local function finish()
		if finished then return end
		finished = true
		introActive = false
		-- уход: знак чуть приближается, стекло проясняется, размытие уходит, меню въезжает
		tween(stack, {Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, -40)}, 0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
		tween(S.uis, {Scale = S.uis.Scale * 1.08}, 0.55, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart)
		tween(ov, {GroupTransparency = 1}, 0.55)
		tween(S.blur, {Size = 0}, 0.55)
		task.delay(0.6, function()
			for _, c in ipairs(iconns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
			ov:Destroy()
			pcall(function() S.blur:Destroy() end)
		end)
		onDone()
	end
	skipB.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() skipped = true; finish() end)
	table.insert(iconns, ov.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
		if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then skipped = true; finish() end
	end))
	table.insert(iconns, UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
		if i.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Space or i.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Return or i.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape then skipped = true; finish() end
	end))

	task.spawn(function()
		local function pause(t)
			local e, T = 0, t * K
			while e < T and not skipped do e += task.wait() end
			return skipped
		end
		-- 1. знак вырастает
		if pause(1.25) then return end
		ring(420, 5)
		sparks()
		-- 2. имя печатается
		local name = "Claude Client"
		local n = utf8.len(name)
		for i = 1, n do
			if skipped then return end
			nameL.Text = name:sub(1, utf8.offset(name, i + 1) - 1)
			task.wait((0.035 + math.random() * 0.035) * K)
		end
		tween(sub, {TextTransparency = 0}, 0.4)
		if pause(0.25) then return end
		-- 3. рамка приветствия
		box.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 62)
		tween(box, {GroupTransparency = 0, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 46)}, 0.45, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart)
		tween(think, {TextTransparency = 0}, 0.4)
		if pause(0.35) then return end
		-- 4. модули грузятся строками
		for i, r in ipairs(S.rows) do
			r.f.Position = r.f.Position + UDim2.fromOffset(-14, 0)
			tween(r.f, {GroupTransparency = 0, Position = r.f.Position + UDim2.fromOffset(14, 0)}, 0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart)
			r.dot.Visible = false
			r.spin.frame.Visible = true
			r.st.Text = "загружаю…"
			local t1 = os.clock()
			if pause(0.16 + math.random() * 0.14) then return end
			r.spin.frame.Visible = false
			r.ok.frame.Visible = true
			r.st.Text = ("%s  (%.2fs)"):format(r.info, (os.clock() - t1) / K)
			r.st.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(245, 240, 232)
		end
		think.Text = "Готово. Хорошей игры, " .. player.DisplayName .. "!"
		if pause(0.3) then return end
		ring(900, 3)
		if pause(0.55) then return end
		finish()
	end)
end

-- ============================ ГЛАВНЫЙ ЦИКЛ ============================
local function updateRocket(dt)
	if unbindCooldown > 0 then unbindCooldown -= dt end

	if activeRocket and (not activeRocket.Parent or not mainPart or not mainPart.Parent) then
		resetCameraToPlayer()
		activeRocket, mainPart, bv, bg = nil, nil, nil, nil
		setFreecam(false)
	end

	-- захват ракеты (запуск только по клику)
	if not activeRocket and unbindCooldown <= 0 then
		local found = findRocketDirectly()
		if found then
			local part = resolvePart(found)
			if part then
				activeRocket, mainPart = found, part
				launched = false
				armedCFrame = mainPart.CFrame

				local look = camera.CFrame.LookVector
				yaw = math.atan2(-look.X, -look.Z)
				pitch = math.asin(math.clamp(look.Y, -1, 1))
				freecamYaw, freecamPitch, lockedYaw, lockedPitch = yaw, pitch, yaw, pitch

				bv = mainPart:FindFirstChild("BodyVelocity") or Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
				bv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
				bv.Velocity = Vector3.zero
				bv.Parent = mainPart

				bg = mainPart:FindFirstChild("BodyGyro") or Instance.new("BodyGyro")
				bg.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
				bg.D, bg.P = 150, 5000
				bg.CFrame = armedCFrame
				bg.Parent = mainPart

				camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable
				resetModeState()
				initialForward = flatten(look)
				wanderDir = initialForward
				ui.hint.Visible = true
			end
		end
	end

	if modeSwitchReset and mainPart and launched then
		resetModeState()
		initialForward = currentForward()
		wanderDir = initialForward
		modeSwitchReset = false
	end

	if not (activeRocket and mainPart and mainPart.Parent) then
		setRkStatus(swarmEnabled and "Статус: включён режим толпы" or (UIK.grab.strict and "Статус: возьми ракету рукой и отпусти — тогда она подключится" or "Статус: ищу ракету (BombMissile) рядом…"))
		return
	end

	if camera.CameraType ~= Enum.CameraType.Scriptable then
		camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable
	end

	local t = os.clock() - startTime
	local camYaw = isFreecam and freecamYaw or yaw
	local camPitch = isFreecam and freecamPitch or pitch
	local camDist = 10

	if not launched then
		setRkStatus("Статус: ракета готова. Кликни по ней ")
		if bv and bv.Parent then bv.Velocity = Vector3.zero end
		if bg and bg.Parent and armedCFrame then bg.CFrame = armedCFrame end
		UIK.rfx.man.rvel, UIK.rfx.man.racc = Vector3.zero, Vector3.zero
		UIK.rfx.update(UIK.rfx.man, mainPart, isFirstPerson) -- дежурный огонёк в сопле
	else
		setRkStatus("Статус: полёт · " .. ROCKET_BY_KEY[rocketModeKey].label)
		local velDir, lookDir, speedOverride = computeMode(dt, t)
		local speed = speedOverride or ui.rkSpeed.Get()
		local mv = UIK.rfx.drive(UIK.rfx.man, mainPart, velDir * speed, math.max(speed * 5, 250))
		if bv and bv.Parent then bv.Velocity = mv end
		local nose = (UIK.rfx.inertia and mv.Magnitude > 8 and velDir.Magnitude > 0.5) and mv.Unit or lookDir -- нос по реальному движению
		if bg and bg.Parent and nose.Magnitude > 0.001 then
			bg.CFrame = CFrame.new(mainPart.Position, mainPart.Position + nose) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-90), 0, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, UIK.rfx.roll(UIK.rfx.man) * 1.2, 0)
		end
		UIK.rfx.update(UIK.rfx.man, mainPart, isFirstPerson)
		camDist += math.clamp(mv.Magnitude / 50, 0, 8) -- на скорости камера отъезжает
		if rocketModeKey == "CINEMATIC" then
			camYaw, camPitch, camDist = t * 0.2, -0.3, 16
		end
	end

	local camRot = CFrame.Angles(0, camYaw, 0) * CFrame.Angles(camPitch, 0, 0)
	local camDir = camRot.LookVector

	if isFirstPerson and not (launched and rocketModeKey == "CINEMATIC") then
		for _, part in ipairs(activeRocket:GetDescendants()) do
			if part:IsA("BasePart") then part.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1 end
		end
		local off = camDir * ((math.max(mainPart.Size.X, mainPart.Size.Y, mainPart.Size.Z) / 2) + 0.5)
		local nose = mainPart.Position + off
		camera.CFrame = CFrame.new(nose, nose + camDir)
	else
		restoreRocketVisibility()
		camera.CFrame = CFrame.new(mainPart.Position) * camRot * CFrame.new(0, 3, camDist) -- 3-е лицо, ракета по центру
	end
	if UIK.rfx.cam and launched then -- ощущение скорости: шире угол обзора и лёгкая дрожь
		local sp = UIK.rfx.man.rvel and UIK.rfx.man.rvel.Magnitude or 0
		UIK.rfx.baseFov = UIK.rfx.baseFov or camera.FieldOfView
		camera.FieldOfView += ((UIK.rfx.baseFov + math.clamp(sp / 400, 0, 1) * 22) - camera.FieldOfView) * math.clamp(dt * 4, 0, 1)
		local amp = math.clamp((sp - 150) / 5000, 0, 0.012)
		if amp > 0 then camera.CFrame = camera.CFrame * CFrame.Angles((math.random() - 0.5) * amp, (math.random() - 0.5) * amp, 0) end
	end
end

local function onRender(dt)
	local hrp = getHRP()
	local hum = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if hrp and hum and hum.Health > 0 and showBodyBox then
		fakeBoxPart.CFrame = hrp.CFrame
		fullBodyBox.Visible = true
	else
		fullBodyBox.Visible = false
	end
	local now = os.clock()
	API.safe("мир", API.worldStep, dt, now)
	API.safe("игроки", API.playersStep, dt, now)
	if ui.footerTick then ui.footerTick(dt) end
	API.safe("захват", UIK.grab.step, dt)
	API.safe("питомец", API.swarmStep, dt, now)
	API.safe("ракета", updateRocket, dt)
	API.safe("спаркеры", updateSparklers, dt, now)
	if UIK.rfx.shakeUntil > now then
		local a = (UIK.rfx.shakeUntil - now) * 1.4
		camera.CFrame = camera.CFrame * CFrame.new((math.random() - 0.5) * a, (math.random() - 0.5) * a, 0)
	end
end

UIK.load(0.9, "Мир, шляпы и баллистика…") -- последний кадр ожидания: дальше главный цикл уже работает
RunService:BindToRenderStep("FembiixMain", Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 1, onRender)

local conns, S = {}, {
	atmo = false, fog = true, fogMul = 1, stars = false, fireflies = false, dust = false, embers = false, aurora = false,
	lightning = false, bolt = 6, hat = "NONE", hatScale = 1, banana = false, bananaMode = "TORNADO",
}
local function c3(r, g, b) return Color3.fromRGB(r, g, b) end
local function mk(class, props, parent)
	local o = Instance.new(class)
	for k, v in pairs(props or {}) do o[k] = v end
	if parent then o.Parent = parent end
	return o
end
local function on(sig, fn) local c = sig:Connect(fn) conns[#conns + 1] = c return c end
local function hrp() local c = lp.Character return c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") end

-- ============================ СВЕТ / АТМОСФЕРА ============================
local PROPS = {"Ambient", "OutdoorAmbient", "Brightness", "ClockTime", "FogColor", "FogEnd", "FogStart", "ExposureCompensation"}
local snap, stash, active, cur = nil, {}, false, nil
local function take()
	if active then return end
	snap = {}
	for _, k in ipairs(PROPS) do snap[k] = Lighting[k] end
	for _, ch in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
		if (ch:IsA("Sky") or ch:IsA("Atmosphere")) and ch.Name:sub(1, 4) ~= "HexA" then
			stash[#stash + 1] = ch
			ch.Parent = nil
		end
	end
	active = true
end
local function give()
	for _, n in ipairs({"HexA_Sky", "HexA_Atmo", "HexA_CC", "HexA_Bloom", "HexA_Rays"}) do
		local o = Lighting:FindFirstChild(n)
		if o then o:Destroy() end
	end
	for _, ch in ipairs(stash) do pcall(function() ch.Parent = Lighting end) end
	stash = {}
	if snap then for k, v in pairs(snap) do pcall(function() Lighting[k] = v end) end end
	snap, active = nil, false
end
local function ensure(class, name)
	local o = Lighting:FindFirstChild(name)
	if not o then o = mk(class, {Name = name}, Lighting) end
	return o
end
local function applyFog()
	if not active or not cur then return end
	local at = ensure("Atmosphere", "HexA_Atmo")
	local m = S.fog and S.fogMul or 0
	at.Density = math.clamp(cur.dens * m, 0, 1)
	at.Haze = cur.haze * m
	Lighting.FogEnd = S.fog and (cur.fogEnd / math.max(S.fogMul, 0.2)) or 100000
	Lighting.FogStart = S.fog and cur.fogStart or 0
end

local lastPreset = PRESETS[1]
local function applyPreset(p)
	p = p or lastPreset
	lastPreset = p
	take()
	cur = p
	Lighting.ClockTime, Lighting.Brightness, Lighting.Ambient, Lighting.OutdoorAmbient, Lighting.FogColor = p.clock, p.br, p.amb, p.out, p.fogC
	local sky = ensure("Sky", "HexA_Sky")
	sky.StarCount, sky.CelestialBodiesShown = p.stars, true
	local at = ensure("Atmosphere", "HexA_Atmo")
	at.Offset, at.Color, at.Decay, at.Glare = 0.25, p.atC, p.decay, p.glare
	local cc = ensure("ColorCorrectionEffect", "HexA_CC")
	cc.TintColor, cc.Saturation, cc.Contrast = p.tint, p.sat, p.con
	local bl = ensure("BloomEffect", "HexA_Bloom")
	bl.Intensity, bl.Size, bl.Threshold = p.bloom, 28, 0.85
	local sr = ensure("SunRaysEffect", "HexA_Rays")
	sr.Intensity, sr.Spread = p.rays, 0.8
	S.atmo = true
	applyFog()
end
local function atmoOff() S.atmo = false give() end

-- ============================ ЧАСТИЦЫ В НЕБЕ И У ЗЕМЛИ ============================
local function seq(...) return NumberSequence.new({...}) end
local function nk(t, v) return NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(t, v) end
local function anchoredPart(size, name)
	return mk("Part", {Name = name, Anchored = true, CanCollide = false, CanQuery = false, CanTouch = false, CastShadow = false, Transparency = 1, Size = size}, fx)
end
local skyP, gndP = anchoredPart(Vector3.new(600, 90, 600), "Sky"), anchoredPart(Vector3.new(80, 16, 80), "Ground")
local E = {}
local function box(part, props)
	props.Shape, props.ShapeStyle = Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box, Enum.ParticleEmitterShapeStyle.Volume
	props.Rate, props.LightEmission, props.Enabled = props.Rate or 10, props.LightEmission or 1, false
	return mk("ParticleEmitter", props, part)
end
E.stars = box(skyP, {Rate = 14, Lifetime = NumberRange.new(8, 14), Speed = NumberRange.new(0, 0), Size = seq(nk(0, 3), nk(1, 3)),

	Transparency = seq(nk(0, 1), nk(0.15, 0.1), nk(0.4, 0.7), nk(0.6, 0.05), nk(0.85, 0.5), nk(1, 1)), Color = ColorSequence.new(c3(200, 255, 110), c3(255, 235, 110)), Acceleration = Vector3.new(0, 0.2, 0)})

	Transparency = seq(nk(0, 1), nk(0.2, 0.55), nk(0.8, 0.55), nk(1, 1)), Color = ColorSequence.new(c3(255, 250, 235)), LightEmission = 0.5})
E.embers = box(gndP, {Rate = 16, Lifetime = NumberRange.new(3, 6), Speed = NumberRange.new(1, 4), SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(35, 35), Size = seq(nk(0, 0.3), nk(1, 0)),
	Transparency = seq(nk(0, 0), nk(0.7, 0.2), nk(1, 1)), Color = ColorSequence.new(c3(255, 210, 90), c3(255, 70, 10)), Acceleration = Vector3.new(0, 4, 0), Rotation = NumberRange.new(0, 360), RotSpeed = NumberRange.new(-90, 90)})
E.embers.EmissionDirection = Enum.NormalId.Top

-- северное сияние: волнистые ленты из Beam
local aur = {}
do
	local cols = {{c3(80, 255, 170), c3(60, 190, 255)}, {c3(120, 255, 140), c3(170, 90, 255)}, {c3(60, 255, 210), c3(90, 140, 255)}, {c3(150, 255, 120), c3(255, 100, 220)}}

		local b = mk("Beam", {Attachment0 = a0, Attachment1 = a1, Width0 = 55, Width1 = 55, Segments = 40, LightEmission = 1, LightInfluence = 0, FaceCamera = true, Enabled = false,
			Color = ColorSequence.new(cols[k][1], cols[k][2]), Transparency = seq(nk(0, 1), nk(0.2, 0.55), nk(0.5, 0.35), nk(0.8, 0.6), nk(1, 1))}, pa)
		aur[k] = {pa = pa, pb = pb, b = b}
	end
end

-- ============================ НАСТОЯЩИЕ МОЛНИИ ============================
local flashCC
local function seg(a, b, th, col, tr)
	local len = math.max((b - a).Magnitude, 0.05)
	return mk("Part", {Anchored = true, CanCollide = false, CanQuery = false, CanTouch = false, CastShadow = false, Material = Enum.Material.Neon,
		Color = col, Transparency = tr or 0, Size = Vector3.new(th, th, len), CFrame = CFrame.lookAt((a + b) / 2, b)}, fx)
end
local function jag(a, b, n, spread)
	local pts = {a}
	for i = 1, n - 1 do
		local f = i / n
		local k = math.sin(f * math.pi) * spread + 1
		pts[#pts + 1] = a:Lerp(b, f) + Vector3.new((math.random() - 0.5) * 2 * k, (math.random() - 0.5) * k * 0.4, (math.random() - 0.5) * 2 * k)
	end
	pts[#pts + 1] = b
	return pts
end
local function flash(power)
	flashCC = flashCC or mk("ColorCorrectionEffect", {Name = "HexA_Flash"}, Lighting)
	flashCC.Brightness = power
	TweenService:Create(flashCC, TweenInfo.new(0.55, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Brightness = 0}):Play()
end
local function drawBolt(a, b, th, life)
	local parts = {}
	local pts = jag(a, b, 16, 16)
	for i = 1, #pts - 1 do
		parts[#parts + 1] = seg(pts[i], pts[i + 1], th, c3(235, 240, 255))
		parts[#parts + 1] = seg(pts[i], pts[i + 1], th * 4.5, c3(150, 175, 255), 0.82)
		if i > 3 and i < #pts - 2 and math.random() < 0.3 then -- ветки
			local e = pts[i] + Vector3.new((math.random() - 0.5) * 70, -math.random(20, 60), (math.random() - 0.5) * 70)
			local br = jag(pts[i], e, 6, 7)
			for j = 1, #br - 1 do parts[#parts + 1] = seg(br[j], br[j + 1], th * 0.45, c3(205, 220, 255), 0.1) end
		end
	end
	local glow = anchoredPart(Vector3.new(1, 1, 1), "BoltLight")
	glow.Position = b + Vector3.new(0, 6, 0)
	mk("PointLight", {Range = 60, Brightness = 6, Color = c3(180, 200, 255)}, glow)
	parts[#parts + 1] = glow
	task.delay(0.07, function() -- мерцание
		for _, p in ipairs(parts) do if p.Parent and p ~= glow then p.Transparency = math.min(p.Transparency + 0.45, 1) end end
	end)
	task.delay(0.14, function()
		for _, p in ipairs(parts) do if p.Parent and p ~= glow then p.Transparency = p.Transparency > 0.7 and 0.15 or p.Transparency end end
	end)
	task.delay(life, function() for _, p in ipairs(parts) do p:Destroy() end end)
end
local function strike()
	local origin = cam.CFrame.Position
	local ang = math.random() * TAU
	local d = 110 + math.random() * 260
	local gx, gz = origin.X + math.cos(ang) * d, origin.Z + math.sin(ang) * d
	local top = Vector3.new(gx + (math.random() - 0.5) * 90, origin.Y + 330, gz + (math.random() - 0.5) * 90)
	local rp = RaycastParams.new()
	rp.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
	rp.FilterDescendantsInstances = {lp.Character, fx}
	local hit = workspace:Raycast(Vector3.new(gx, origin.Y + 400, gz), Vector3.new(0, -900, 0), rp)
	local ground = hit and hit.Position or Vector3.new(gx, origin.Y - 4, gz)
	local th = 0.7 + math.random() * 0.6
	drawBolt(top, ground, th, 0.45)
	if math.random() < 0.4 then task.delay(0.12, function() drawBolt(top, ground + Vector3.new(math.random(-6, 6), 0, math.random(-6, 6)), th * 0.8, 0.35) end) end
	flash(0.28 + math.random() * 0.2)
	if active then
		local base = snap and snap.ExposureCompensation or 0
		Lighting.ExposureCompensation = base + 1.8
		TweenService:Create(Lighting, TweenInfo.new(0.5), {ExposureCompensation = base}):Play()
	end
end

-- ============================ ШЛЯПЫ (детальные, с эффектами) ============================
local function cylP(r, h, y, col, mat, x, z) return {Enum.PartType.Cylinder, Vector3.new(h, r * 2, r * 2), CFrame.new(x or 0, y, z or 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.pi / 2), col, mat or Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic} end
local function blkP(size, cf, col, mat, shape, extra)
	local t = {shape or Enum.PartType.Block, size, cf, col, mat or Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic}
	for k, v in pairs(extra or {}) do t[k] = v end
	return t
end
local function sparkle(col1, col2, rate)
	return function(p)
		mk("ParticleEmitter", {Rate = rate or 8, Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.6, 1.3), Speed = NumberRange.new(0.5, 2), SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(180, 180), LightEmission = 1,
			Size = seq(nk(0, 0.35), nk(1, 0)), Transparency = seq(nk(0, 0), nk(1, 1)), Color = ColorSequence.new(col1, col2 or col1), Rotation = NumberRange.new(0, 360)}, p)
	end
end
local HATS = {}
HATS.WIZARD = function()
	local l = {cylP(2.7, 0.14, 0.05, c3(48, 24, 85), Enum.Material.Fabric), cylP(2.15, 0.1, 0.18, c3(62, 30, 105), Enum.Material.Fabric)}
	for i = 0, 15 do -- конус с загнутым кончиком
		local f = i / 16
		l[#l + 1] = cylP(1.7 * (1 - f) + 0.06, 0.4, 0.25 + i * 0.36, (i % 2 == 0) and c3(60, 28, 100) or c3(70, 34, 112), Enum.Material.Fabric, f * f * 1.7)
	end
	l[#l + 1] = cylP(1.74, 0.28, 0.5, c3(255, 190, 40), Enum.Material.Metal)
	l[#l + 1] = blkP(Vector3.new(0.5, 0.42, 0.12), CFrame.new(0, 0.5, -1.72), c3(255, 225, 90), Enum.Material.Neon)
	for i = 1, 4 do
		l[#l + 1] = blkP(Vector3.new(0.34, 0.34, 0.34), CFrame.new(2.3, 1.3 + i * 0.35, 0), (i % 2 == 0) and c3(255, 235, 120) or c3(150, 220, 255), Enum.Material.Neon, Enum.PartType.Ball,
			{orb = 1.4 + i * 0.3, ph = i * 1.57, fx = sparkle(c3(255, 235, 140), c3(180, 140, 255), 4)})
	end
	l[#l + 1] = blkP(Vector3.new(0.35, 0.35, 0.35), CFrame.new(1.7 * 1.0, 6.2, 0), c3(255, 240, 160), Enum.Material.Neon, Enum.PartType.Ball, {fx = sparkle(c3(255, 240, 160), c3(200, 120, 255), 14)})
	return l
end
HATS.CHINA = function()
	local l = {}
	for i = 0, 17 do
		local f = i / 18
		local straw = (i % 3 == 0) and c3(190, 150, 80) or ((i % 2 == 0) and c3(230, 198, 125) or c3(215, 180, 105))
		l[#l + 1] = cylP(3.7 * (1 - f) + 0.05, 0.13, 0.08 + i * 0.15, straw, Enum.Material.Fabric)
	end
	l[#l + 1] = cylP(3.72, 0.1, 0.04, c3(140, 28, 28), Enum.Material.Fabric) -- красный кант по краю
	l[#l + 1] = cylP(1.05, 0.12, 0.16, c3(150, 25, 25), Enum.Material.Fabric)
	l[#l + 1] = blkP(Vector3.new(0.62, 0.62, 0.62), CFrame.new(0, 2.78, 0), c3(215, 35, 35), Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic, Enum.PartType.Ball)
	l[#l + 1] = blkP(Vector3.new(0.22, 0.22, 0.22), CFrame.new(0, 3.15, 0), c3(255, 205, 70), Enum.Material.Metal, Enum.PartType.Ball)
	for i = 1, 6 do -- кисточка
		local a = i / 6 * TAU
		l[#l + 1] = blkP(Vector3.new(0.05, 1.1, 0.05), CFrame.new(math.cos(a) * 0.22, 2.1, math.sin(a) * 0.22), c3(200, 30, 30), Enum.Material.Fabric, nil, {sway = i})
	end
	return l
end
HATS.CROWN = function()
	local l = {cylP(1.15, 0.28, 0.14, c3(255, 200, 40), Enum.Material.Metal), cylP(1.08, 0.7, 0.5, c3(170, 25, 45), Enum.Material.Fabric), cylP(1.2, 0.2, 0.86, c3(255, 205, 50), Enum.Material.Metal)}
	local gem = {c3(255, 50, 80), c3(70, 170, 255), c3(90, 255, 150)}
	for i = 0, 5 do
		local a = i / 6 * TAU
		local x, z = math.cos(a) * 1.15, math.sin(a) * 1.15
		l[#l + 1] = blkP(Vector3.new(0.34, 1.1, 0.34), CFrame.new(x, 1.35, z) * CFrame.Angles(-math.sin(a) * 0.18, 0, math.cos(a) * 0.18), c3(255, 210, 60), Enum.Material.Metal)
		l[#l + 1] = blkP(Vector3.new(0.32, 0.32, 0.32), CFrame.new(x * 1.06, 2.0, z * 1.06), gem[i % 3 + 1], Enum.Material.Neon, Enum.PartType.Ball, {fx = sparkle(gem[i % 3 + 1], c3(255, 255, 255), 5)})
		l[#l + 1] = blkP(Vector3.new(0.3, 0.3, 0.3), CFrame.new(math.cos(a) * 1.18, 0.14, math.sin(a) * 1.18), gem[(i + 1) % 3 + 1], Enum.Material.Neon, Enum.PartType.Ball)
	end
	return l
end
HATS.HALO = function()
	local l = {}
	for i = 0, 31 do
		local a = i / 32 * TAU
		l[#l + 1] = blkP(Vector3.new(0.3, 0.2, 0.62), CFrame.new(math.cos(a) * 1.7, 2.3, math.sin(a) * 1.7) * CFrame.Angles(0, -a, 0), c3(255, 238, 150), Enum.Material.Neon)
		l[#l + 1] = blkP(Vector3.new(0.7, 0.55, 0.9), CFrame.new(math.cos(a) * 1.7, 2.3, math.sin(a) * 1.7) * CFrame.Angles(0, -a, 0), c3(255, 225, 120), Enum.Material.Neon, nil, {tr = 0.85})
	end
	l[#l + 1] = blkP(Vector3.new(0.2, 0.2, 0.2), CFrame.new(0, 2.3, 0), c3(255, 240, 170), Enum.Material.Neon, Enum.PartType.Ball, {fx = function(p)
		mk("PointLight", {Range = 20, Brightness = 2.5, Color = c3(255, 235, 150)}, p)
		sparkle(c3(255, 240, 170), c3(255, 200, 90), 10)(p)
	end})
	return l
end
HATS.HORNS = function()
	local l = {}
	for _, side in ipairs({-1, 1}) do
		for i = 0, 7 do
			local f = i / 8
			local x, y = side * (0.55 + f * 1.1 + f * f * 0.5), 0.2 + f * 1.9 - f * f * 0.5
			local th = 0.5 * (1 - f) + 0.08
			local isTip = (i == 7)
			l[#l + 1] = blkP(Vector3.new(th, 0.42, th), CFrame.new(x, y, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, -side * (0.5 + f * 0.9)), isTip and c3(255, 90, 30) or c3(130, 18, 28), isTip and Enum.Material.Neon or Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic,
				nil, isTip and {fx = function(p) mk("ParticleEmitter", {Rate = 14, Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.5, 1), Speed = NumberRange.new(1, 3), SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(25, 25), LightEmission = 1, Size = seq(nk(0, 0.35), nk(1, 0)), Transparency = seq(nk(0, 0.1), nk(1, 1)), Color = ColorSequence.new(c3(255, 200, 60), c3(255, 40, 0)), Acceleration = Vector3.new(0, 4, 0)}, p) end} or nil)
		end
	end
	return l
end
HATS.TOP = function()
	return {cylP(1.1, 1.9, 1.0, c3(18, 18, 22)), cylP(1.95, 0.14, 0.07, c3(18, 18, 22)), cylP(1.12, 0.4, 0.4, c3(190, 25, 50), Enum.Material.Fabric),
		blkP(Vector3.new(0.4, 0.36, 0.1), CFrame.new(0, 0.4, -1.13), c3(255, 210, 70), Enum.Material.Metal),
		blkP(Vector3.new(0.3, 0.3, 0.3), CFrame.new(0.6, 2.1, 0.2), c3(255, 255, 255), Enum.Material.Neon, Enum.PartType.Ball, {fx = sparkle(c3(255, 255, 255), c3(180, 200, 255), 6)})}
end
HATS.PUMPKIN = function()
	local l = {blkP(Vector3.new(2.7, 2.2, 2.7), CFrame.new(0, 1.0, 0), c3(255, 120, 20), Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic, Enum.PartType.Ball), blkP(Vector3.new(0.4, 0.8, 0.4), CFrame.new(0, 2.25, 0), c3(70, 130, 45)),
		blkP(Vector3.new(0.9, 0.6, 0.3), CFrame.new(-0.55, 1.2, -1.28), c3(255, 220, 70), Enum.Material.Neon), blkP(Vector3.new(0.9, 0.6, 0.3), CFrame.new(0.55, 1.2, -1.28), c3(255, 220, 70), Enum.Material.Neon),
		blkP(Vector3.new(1.4, 0.35, 0.3), CFrame.new(0, 0.5, -1.3), c3(255, 220, 70), Enum.Material.Neon, nil, {fx = function(p) mk("PointLight", {Range = 18, Brightness = 2, Color = c3(255, 160, 40)}, p) end})}
	for i = 0, 4 do l[#l + 1] = blkP(Vector3.new(0.12, 2.1, 0.12), CFrame.new(0, 1.0, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, i / 5 * math.pi, 0) * CFrame.new(1.28, 0, 0), c3(215, 95, 10)) end
	return l
end
local HAT_NAMES = {{"NONE", "Без шляпы"}, {"WIZARD", "Волшебная"}, {"CHINA", "Китайская"}, {"CROWN", "Корона"}, {"HALO", "Нимб"}, {"HORNS", "Рога"}, {"TOP", "Цилиндр"}, {"PUMPKIN", "Тыква"}}
local hatParts = {}
local function clearHat() for _, h in ipairs(hatParts) do h.part:Destroy() end hatParts = {} end
local function buildHat()
	clearHat()
	local f = HATS[S.hat]
	if not f then return end
	local sc = S.hatScale
	for _, d in ipairs(f()) do
		local tr = d.tr
		local p = mk("Part", {Name = "HatPart", Shape = d[1], Size = d[2] * sc, Color = d[4], Material = d[5], Anchored = true, CanCollide = false, CanQuery = false, CanTouch = false, CastShadow = false, Transparency = tr or 0}, fx)
		if d.fx then d.fx(p) end
		local off = d[3]
		hatParts[#hatParts + 1] = {part = p, off = CFrame.new(off.Position * sc) * (off - off.Position), orb = d.orb, ph = d.ph or 0, sway = d.sway, baseTr = tr or 0}
	end
end

-- ============================ БАНАНЫ: «БАНАНОВАЯ ВСЕЛЕННАЯ» ============================
local bananas = {}
local function bananaFolder() return workspace:FindFirstChild(lp.Name .. "SpawnedInToys") end
local function releaseBanana(b)
	UIK.grab.forget(b.obj)
	for _, o in ipairs({b.ap, b.ao, b.att, b.tr, b.a0, b.a1, b.pl}) do pcall(function() o:Destroy() end) end
	for p, c in pairs(b.cc) do if p.Parent then p.CanCollide = c end end
end
local function releaseBananas() for _, b in ipairs(bananas) do releaseBanana(b) end bananas = {} end
local function captureBananas()
	local folder, root = bananaFolder(), hrp()
	if not folder or not root then return end
	local have = {}
	for _, b in ipairs(bananas) do have[b.obj] = true end
	for _, ch in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
		if #bananas >= 30 then break end
		if ch.Name == "FoodBanana" and not have[ch] and UIK.grab.ok(ch) then
			local part = ch:IsA("BasePart") and ch or (ch.PrimaryPart or ch:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true))
			if part and (part.Position - root.Position).Magnitude < 60 then
				local b = {obj = ch, part = part, cc = {}, i = #bananas + 1, ph = math.random() * TAU}
				local list = ch:IsA("BasePart") and {ch} or {}
				for _, d in ipairs(ch:GetDescendants()) do if d:IsA("BasePart") then list[#list + 1] = d end end
				for _, p in ipairs(list) do b.cc[p] = p.CanCollide p.CanCollide = false end
				b.att = mk("Attachment", {}, part)
				b.ap = mk("AlignPosition", {Mode = Enum.PositionAlignmentMode.OneAttachment, Attachment0 = b.att, MaxForce = math.huge, Responsiveness = 22, ApplyAtCenterOfMass = true}, part)
				b.ao = mk("AlignOrientation", {Mode = Enum.OrientationAlignmentMode.OneAttachment, Attachment0 = b.att, MaxTorque = math.huge, Responsiveness = 18}, part)
				b.a0, b.a1 = mk("Attachment", {Position = Vector3.new(0, 0.5, 0)}, part), mk("Attachment", {Position = Vector3.new(0, -0.5, 0)}, part)
				b.tr = mk("Trail", {Attachment0 = b.a0, Attachment1 = b.a1, Lifetime = 0.6, LightEmission = 1, FaceCamera = true, Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0.1, 1), Color = ColorSequence.new(c3(255, 235, 80), c3(255, 160, 20))}, part)
				b.pl = mk("PointLight", {Range = 9, Brightness = 1.2, Color = c3(255, 225, 90)}, part)
				bananas[#bananas + 1] = b
			end
		end
	end
end
local function bananaPos(b, n, t, P)
	local u = (b.i - 1) / math.max(n, 1)
	if S.bananaMode == "TORNADO" then -- воронка из бананов вокруг тебя, внизу узко, вверху широко
		local h = ((u + t * 0.07) % 1)
		local a = t * (3.5 - h * 2) + b.i * 2.4
		return P + Vector3.new(math.cos(a) * (1.8 + h * 9), -2 + h * 15, math.sin(a) * (1.8 + h * 9)), Vector3.new(-math.sin(a), 0.5, math.cos(a))
	elseif S.bananaMode == "ORBITS" then -- банановая солнечная система, у каждого своя наклонная орбита
		local r = 4 + b.i * 1.15
		local a = t * (2.6 / (1 + r * 0.12)) + b.ph
		local rot = CFrame.Angles(b.ph * 0.15, b.i * 0.7, b.ph * 0.1)
		return P + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0) + rot:VectorToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(math.cos(a) * r, 0, math.sin(a) * r)), Vector3.new(-math.sin(a), 0, math.cos(a))
	else -- SALUTE: банановый салют — взрыв наружу, зависание, схлопывание обратно
		local ph = (t / 4.5) % 1
		local y = 1 - 2 * ((b.i - 0.5) / math.max(n, 1))
		local rr = math.sqrt(math.max(0, 1 - y * y))
		local th = b.i * 2.39996
		local d = Vector3.new(math.cos(th) * rr, y, math.sin(th) * rr)
		local e = (ph < 0.3) and (1 - (1 - ph / 0.3) ^ 3) or ((ph < 0.75) and 1 or (1 - ((ph - 0.75) / 0.25) ^ 2))
		return P + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0) + d * (1.5 + 14 * e), d
	end
end
local function bananaStep(t)
	if not S.banana then return end
	local root = hrp()
	if not root then return end
	local n = #bananas
	for i = n, 1, -1 do
		if not bananas[i].part.Parent then releaseBanana(table.remove(bananas, i)) end
	end
	n = #bananas
	for i, b in ipairs(bananas) do b.i = i end
	for _, b in ipairs(bananas) do
		local pos, dir = bananaPos(b, n, t, root.Position)
		b.ap.Position = pos + Vector3.new(0, math.sin(t * 3 + b.ph) * 0.4, 0)
		local up = (dir.Magnitude > 0.01) and dir.Unit or Vector3.yAxis
		b.ao.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(Vector3.zero, up) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(90), t * 4 + b.ph, 0) -- кувыркаются на ходу
	end
end

-- ============================ ГЛАВНЫЙ ЦИКЛ ============================
local nextBolt, capT = 0, 0
local function onRender(dt)
	local t = os.clock()
	local c = cam.CFrame.Position
	skyP.Position = c + Vector3.new(0, 130, 0)
	local r = hrp()
	if r then gndP.Position = r.Position + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0) end
	for k, e in pairs(E) do e.Enabled = S[k] == true end
	if S.aurora then
		for k, a in ipairs(aur) do
			a.b.Enabled = true
			local z = -300 + k * 18
			a.pa.Position = Vector3.new(c.X - 190, c.Y + 190 + k * 14 + math.sin(t * 0.5 + k) * 14, c.Z + z)
			a.pb.Position = Vector3.new(c.X + 190, c.Y + 190 + k * 14 + math.cos(t * 0.4 + k * 2) * 14, c.Z + z + math.sin(t * 0.3 + k) * 40)
			a.b.CurveSize0, a.b.CurveSize1 = math.sin(t * 0.6 + k) * 70, math.cos(t * 0.5 + k * 1.3) * 70
			a.b.Width0 = 45 + math.sin(t * 0.8 + k) * 15
			a.b.Width1 = 45 + math.cos(t * 0.7 + k) * 15
		end
	else
		for _, a in ipairs(aur) do a.b.Enabled = false end
	end
	if S.lightning and t > nextBolt then
		nextBolt = t + (S.bolt * 0.5) + math.random() * S.bolt
		strike()
		if math.random() < 0.3 then task.delay(0.35, strike) end
	end
	if #hatParts > 0 then
		local hd = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
		if hd then
			local base = hd.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, hd.Size.Y / 2 + math.sin(t * 2) * 0.05, 0)
			local fp = (c - hd.Position).Magnitude < 2
			for _, h in ipairs(hatParts) do
				local cf = h.off
				if h.orb then cf = CFrame.Angles(0, t * h.orb + h.ph, 0) * cf end
				if h.sway then cf = cf * CFrame.Angles(math.sin(t * 3 + h.sway) * 0.12, 0, math.cos(t * 2.4 + h.sway) * 0.12) end
				h.part.CFrame = base * cf
				h.part.Transparency = fp and 1 or h.baseTr
			end
		end
	end
	capT -= dt
	if S.banana and capT <= 0 then capT = 1 captureBananas() end
end
on(RunService.RenderStepped, onRender)
on(RunService.Heartbeat, function() bananaStep(os.clock()) end)

-- ============================ ИНТЕРФЕЙС ============================
local BG, PN, TX, DM, WH = C.bg, C.el, C.text, C.dim, C.white
local gui = mk("ScreenGui", {Name = "HexAddonGui", ResetOnSpawn = false, DisplayOrder = 60, IgnoreGuiInset = true, ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling})
pcall(function() gui.Parent = CoreGui end)
if not gui.Parent then gui.Parent = lp:WaitForChild("PlayerGui") end
local win = mk("Frame", {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(300, 430), Position = UDim2.new(1, -316, 0.5, -215), BackgroundColor3 = BG, BackgroundTransparency = 0.1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Active = true}, gui)
mk("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)}, win)
mk("UIStroke", {Color = C.line, Transparency = 0.88}, win)
local title = mk("TextLabel", {Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, 32), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "Claude Client  ·  атмосфера и эффекты", Font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono, TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = WH, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left}, win)
local closeB = mk("TextButton", {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(26, 26), Position = UDim2.new(1, -30, 0, 3), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "x", Font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono, TextSize = 14, TextColor3 = DM}, win)
local list = mk("ScrollingFrame", {Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 1, -38), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 34), BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, ScrollBarThickness = 2, CanvasSize = UDim2.new(), AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y}, win)
mk("UIListLayout", {Padding = UDim.new(0, 4), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder}, list)
local ord = 0
local function nx() ord += 1 return ord end
local function head(text) mk("TextLabel", {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = text, Font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono, TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = WH, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, LayoutOrder = nx()}, list) end
local function btn(text, cb, col)
	local b = mk("TextButton", {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28), BackgroundColor3 = col or PN, BackgroundTransparency = 0.3, BorderSizePixel = 0, AutoButtonColor = true, Text = text, Font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono, TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = TX, LayoutOrder = nx()}, list)
	mk("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)}, b)
	b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(cb)
	return b
end
local function tog(text, key, cb)
	local b = mk("TextButton", {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", LayoutOrder = nx()}, list)
	mk("TextLabel", {Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 1, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = text, Font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono, TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = TX, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left}, b)
	local sw = mk("Frame", {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 14), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -2, 0.5, 0), BackgroundColor3 = c3(58, 58, 63), BorderSizePixel = 0}, b)
	mk("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)}, sw)
	local kn = mk("Frame", {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 10), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, 0), BackgroundColor3 = DM, BorderSizePixel = 0}, sw)
	mk("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)}, kn)
	local function paint()
		local v = S[key]
		TweenService:Create(sw, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {BackgroundColor3 = v and C.accent or c3(66, 64, 60)}):Play()
		TweenService:Create(kn, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {Position = v and UDim2.new(1, -12, 0.5, 0) or UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, 0), BackgroundColor3 = v and BG or DM}):Play()
	end
	b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() S[key] = not S[key] paint() if cb then cb(S[key]) end end)
	paint()
	return paint
end
local function step(text, key, mn, mx, st, cb)
	local row = mk("Frame", {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26), BackgroundTransparency = 1, LayoutOrder = nx()}, list)
	local lbl = mk("TextLabel", {Size = UDim2.new(1, -90, 1, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono, TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = TX, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left}, row)
	local function paint() lbl.Text = text .. ": " .. tostring(math.floor(S[key] * 100 + 0.5) / 100) end
	local function mb(sym, x, d)
		local b = mk("TextButton", {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(34, 22), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, x, 0.5, 0), BackgroundColor3 = PN, BorderSizePixel = 0, Text = sym, Font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono, TextSize = 14, TextColor3 = WH}, row)
		mk("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)}, b)
		b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() S[key] = math.clamp(S[key] + d * st, mn, mx) paint() if cb then cb(S[key]) end end)
	end
	mb("+", 0, 1)
	mb("-", -38, -1)
	paint()
end

tog("Туман", "fog", function() applyFog() end)

tog("Северное сияние", "aurora")
tog("Светлячки", "fireflies")
tog("Пылинки в воздухе", "dust")

for _, h in ipairs(HAT_NAMES) do btn(h[2], function() S.hat = h[1] buildHat() end) end

head("Бананы")

btn("Режим: Воронка", function() S.bananaMode = "TORNADO" end)
btn("Режим: Орбиты-планеты", function() S.bananaMode = "ORBITS" end)
btn("Режим: Банановый салют", function() S.bananaMode = "SALUTE" end)
head("Прочее")
btn("Вернуть свет игры", function() atmoOff() paintAtmo() end)
local function stop()
	for _, c in ipairs(conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
	releaseBananas()
	give()
	if flashCC then flashCC:Destroy() end
	fx:Destroy()
	gui:Destroy()
	_G.HexAddonStop = nil
end
_G.HexAddonStop = stop
btn("Выгрузить аддон", stop, c3(92, 44, 48))
function API.atmoSunset()
	for _, p in ipairs(PRESETS) do
		if p.n == "Закат" then applyPreset(p) end
	end
	paintAtmo()
end
API.atmoSunset() -- конфиг Claude: атмосфера «Закат» с самого начала
closeB.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() win.Visible = false end)

-- кнопка на экране + перетаскивание + клавиша
local open = mk("TextButton", {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(40, 40), Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0.5, 40), BackgroundColor3 = BG, BackgroundTransparency = 0.15, Text = "", Font = Enum.Font.RobotoMono, TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = WH, BorderSizePixel = 0}, gui)
Icon("claude", open, 22, C.accent, {AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)})
mk("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)}, open)
mk("UIStroke", {Color = C.line, Transparency = 0.75}, open)
open.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() win.Visible = not win.Visible end)
on(UIS.InputBegan, function(i, gp) if not gp and i.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.RightControl then win.Visible = not win.Visible end end)
do
	local drag, ds, sp
	title.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
		if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			drag, ds, sp = true, i.Position, win.Position
			i.Changed:Connect(function() if i.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then drag = false end end)
		end
	end)
	on(UIS.InputChanged, function(i)
		if drag and (i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
			local d = i.Position - ds
			win.Position = UDim2.new(sp.X.Scale, sp.X.Offset + d.X, sp.Y.Scale, sp.Y.Offset + d.Y)
		end
	end)
end
win.Visible = false -- окно аддона открывается кнопкой с логотипом слева или RightControl
end)
if not okA then warn("[Claude] аддон: " .. tostring(errA)) end

-- ============================ ВСТРОЕННАЯ БАЛЛИСТИКА v4 ============================
API.balOk, API.balErr = pcall(function()
	local CCN, CCD, CCUIK, CCAPI = notify, C.danger, UIK, API
--[[ =====================================================================================
  HEXAGRAM BALLISTICS v4 · 3D · pastel — отдельный модуль баллистики (FTAP)

  ГЛАВНОЕ В v4
   • Поддоны (WhiteBrownPallet) — это строительные блоки. Каждый поддон держится физикой
     (BodyVelocity + BodyGyro), поэтому конструкции видят все и они реально двигаются:
     башня турели крутится, ствол поднимается, рельса пусковой задирается на угол пуска.
   • Настоящая баллистика: решение по гравитации (апогей → время полёта → вектор пуска),
     наведение в полёте по формуле «попасть в точку за оставшееся время», упреждение
     движущихся игроков, финальный пикирующий удар с ограничением угловой скорости.
   • 3D-визуализация: пунктир траектории, кольцо попадания, лазер ствола, кольца радиусов,
     3D-табличка над установкой с зарядами/углом/временем полёта.

  РЕЖИМЫ
   • Баллистика — связка ракет на поворотной пусковой с подъёмной рельсой, бьёт по дуге
   • Орбита     — то же, но апогей очень высокий: почти отвесное падение
   • Веер       — в апогее связка расходится и каждая ракета бьёт в свою точку кольца
   • Турель     — 3D-турель из поддонов: основание, поворотная башня, ствол со стволовой коробкой,
                  ракеты на направляющих сверху. Сама ищет чужих, наводится с упреждением,
                  проверяет линию огня и стреляет самонаводящимися ракетами. Есть ручной режим.
   • ПВО        — «Патриот» из 12 поддонов: прицеп, погон, пусковой контейнер на 4 перехватчика.
                  Ловит чужие ракеты в полёте (и швырнутых игроков), бьёт залпом в точку встречи.
   • Стройка    — постройки из поддонов: Куб, Стена, Башня, Лестница, Танк (башня танка следит за прицелом)

  Управление (ПК):   клик по ракете/поддону — выбрать/убрать · F — цель (или выстрел в ручной турели)
                     X коротко — поставить / убрать установку · X держать 2 с — ПУСК
                     в полёте X — прервать · RightShift / H — скрыть окно · End — всё сбросить
  Сенсор:            тап по ракете/поддону · круглые кнопки F и X
===================================================================================== ]]
if shared.HexBalCleanup then pcall(shared.HexBalCleanup) end

local function make(class, props, parent)
	local o = Instance.new(class)
	for k, v in pairs(props or {}) do o[k] = v end
	if parent then o.Parent = parent end
	return o
end
local function round(o, r) return make("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, r)}, o) end
local function stroke(o, color, th, tr)
	return make("UIStroke", {Color = color, Thickness = th or 1, Transparency = tr or 0, ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border}, o)
end
local function tween(o, props, t, style, dir)
	local tw = TweenService:Create(o, TweenInfo.new(t or 0.15, style or Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, dir or Enum.EasingDirection.Out), props)
	tw:Play()
	return tw
end
local conns = {}
local function connect(sig, fn)
	local c = sig:Connect(fn)
	conns[#conns + 1] = c
	return c
end
local function flatten(v)
	local f = Vector3.new(v.X, 0, v.Z)
	if f.Magnitude < 0.001 then return Vector3.new(0, 0, -1) end
	return f.Unit
end
local function safeUnit(v, fallback)
	if v.Magnitude < 1e-5 then return fallback end
	return v.Unit
end
local function sm(k)
	k = math.clamp(k, 0, 1)
	return k * k * (3 - 2 * k)
end
local function angWrap(a) return (a + math.pi) % TAU - math.pi end
local function yawOf(d) return math.atan2(-d.X, -d.Z) end
local function angleBetween(a, b) return math.acos(math.clamp(a:Dot(b), -1, 1)) end
-- повернуть единичный вектор a к b не больше чем на maxAng (сферически)
local function rotateToward(a, b, maxAng)
	local ang = angleBetween(a, b)
	if ang <= maxAng or ang < 1e-4 then return b end
	local s = math.sin(ang)
	if s < 1e-3 then return safeUnit(a:Lerp(b, maxAng / ang) + Vector3.new(0, 1e-3, 0), b) end
	local k = maxAng / ang
	return ((a * math.sin((1 - k) * ang) + b * math.sin(k * ang)) / s).Unit
end
local function resolvePart(obj)
	if obj:IsA("BasePart") then return obj end
	-- в папке игрушек лежат и служебные объекты (NumberValue «ToyNumber» и т.п.) — у них нет PrimaryPart
	if obj:IsA("Model") and obj.PrimaryPart then return obj.PrimaryPart end
	return obj:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
end
local function getHRP()
	local c = player.Character
	return c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
end
local function myFolders()
	local SUFFIX = "SpawnedInToys"
	local SLEN = #SUFFIX
	local list, seen, nm, dn = {}, {}, player.Name, player.DisplayName
	local function add(o)
		if o and not seen[o] then
			seen[o] = true
			list[#list + 1] = o
		end
	end
	add(CCAPI.plotFolder) -- режим «Плот»: папка PlotItems/PlotN, где ты стоишь
	add(workspace:FindFirstChild(nm .. SUFFIX))
	add(workspace:FindFirstChild(dn .. SUFFIX))
	for _, ch in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
		if (ch:IsA("Folder") or ch:IsA("Model")) and ch.Name:sub(-SLEN) == SUFFIX then
			local pref = ch.Name:sub(1, #ch.Name - SLEN)
			if pref == nm or pref == dn then add(ch) end
		end
	end
	if #list == 0 then
		for _, ch in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
			if (ch:IsA("Folder") or ch:IsA("Model")) and ch.Name:sub(-SLEN) == SUFFIX then add(ch) end
		end
	end
	return list
end
local function looksLikeRocket(obj)
	local ROCKET_WORDS = {"bombmissile", "missile", "rocket", "bomb"}
	local n = obj.Name:lower()
	for _, w in ipairs(ROCKET_WORDS) do
		if n:find(w, 1, true) then return true end
	end
	return false
end
local function isPallet(obj)
	local n = obj.Name:lower()
	return n:find("pallet", 1, true) ~= nil or n:find("pellet", 1, true) ~= nil
end
-- поставить объект так, чтобы его часть part оказалась в cf
local function placeObj(obj, part, cf)
	if obj:IsA("Model") then
		obj:PivotTo(cf * part.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(obj:GetPivot()))
	else
		part.CFrame = cf
	end
end
local function bodyParts(obj)
	local list = obj:IsA("BasePart") and {obj} or {}
	for _, d in ipairs(obj:GetDescendants()) do
		if d:IsA("BasePart") then list[#list + 1] = d end
	end
	return list
end
local function playerFromInstance(inst)
	local m = inst and inst:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
	while m do
		local pl = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(m)
		if pl then return pl end
		m = m.Parent and m.Parent:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
	end
	return nil
end
local function playerHRP(p)
	local c = p and p.Character
	return c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
end
-- точка упреждения: где будет цель, когда до неё долетит снаряд со скоростью s
local function intercept(from, p, v, s)
	local t = (p - from).Magnitude / math.max(s, 1)
	for _ = 1, 3 do t = ((p + v * t) - from).Magnitude / math.max(s, 1) end
	t = math.min(t, 3)
	return p + Vector3.new(v.X, math.clamp(v.Y, -30, 30) * 0.35, v.Z) * t
end
